Mer exempel

create database konhri_snapshot_20180531_232323 on
(name = konhri, filename = 'W:\sqldba\data\konhri_snapshot.ss')
as snapshot of konhri

declare @snapshotname nvarchar(255)
declare @drop nvarchar(100)
declare @runcommand nvarchar(100)
set @drop = 'drop database '
select @snapshotname = name from sys.databases where source_database_id is not null and name like 'konhri%snapshot%'
set @runcommand = (@drop + @snapshotname)
exec sp_executesql @runcommand

Let user drop snapshots via proxy account, also renaming
USE [master]
GO
CREATE LOGIN [renamedb_proxy] WITH PASSWORD=N'XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX', DEFAULT_DATABASE=[master], CHECK_EXPIRATION=OFF, CHECK_POLICY=ON
GO
USE msdb
GO
CREATE USER [renamedb_proxy] FOR LOGIN [renamedb_proxy]
GO
ALTER SERVER ROLE [dbcreator] ADD MEMBER [renamedb_proxy]
GO


USE msdb
GO
CREATE  PROCEDURE dbo.RenameEtelBOSystest 
-- Rename database EtelBOSystest as EtelBOSystestBackup on sesoco2285
-- also remove snapshot if it exists
 WITH EXECUTE AS 'renamedb_proxy' 
   AS
	DECLARE @snapshotname NVARCHAR(255)
	DECLARE @drop NVARCHAR(100)
	DECLARE @runcommand NVARCHAR(100)

	IF EXISTS(SELECT NAME FROM sys.databases WHERE source_database_id is not null AND NAME LIKE 'EtelBOSystest%snapshot%')
	BEGIN
		SET @drop = 'drop database '
		SELECT @snapshotname = NAME FROM sys.databases WHERE source_database_id is not null AND NAME LIKE 'EtelBOSystest%snapshot%'
		SET @runcommand = (@drop + @snapshotname)
		EXEC sp_executesql @runcommand;
	END
	ELSE
		SELECT 'No snapshot found of EtelBOSystest'

	ALTER DATABASE EtelBOSystest SET SINGLE_USER WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE;
	ALTER DATABASE EtelBOSystest MODIFY NAME = EtelBOSystestBackup
	ALTER DATABASE EtelBOSystestBackup SET MULTI_USER WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE
GO

GRANT EXECUTE ON msdb.dbo.RenameEtelBOSystest TO [GLOBAL\serviceetelbobuild];
GO

Database snapshots with AlwaysOn, example

CREATE DATABASE SalesQuotation_snapshot
    ON 
	( 
	NAME = SalesQuotationPreProd, --logical filename of source data file
	FILENAME = 'W:\SQLDba\Data\SalesQuotation_snapshot.ss'
	)
    AS SNAPSHOT OF SalesQuotation


--Drop database snapshot if no longer needed
DROP DATABASE SalesQuotation_snapshot


-- Suspend data movement of the database in Availability group.
ALTER DATABASE SalesQuotation SET HADR SUSPEND
 
-- Remove the database from Availability group
ALTER AVAILABILITY GROUP sqlxag05 REMOVE DATABASE SalesQuotation; 
 
-- Change the status of database to Single user mode
ALTER DATABASE SalesQuotation SET SINGLE_USER WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE;

--Restore from database snapshot
RESTORE DATABASE SalesQuotation FROM DATABASE_SNAPSHOT = 'SalesQuotation_snapshot'

Example of job to recreate snapshot on a schedule

USE [msdb]
GO

/****** Object:  Job [Re-create Ax2012SlupskTEST snapshot]    Script Date: 2/1/2016 8:59:08 AM ******/
BEGIN TRANSACTION
DECLARE @ReturnCode INT
SELECT @ReturnCode = 0
/****** Object:  JobCategory [[Uncategorized (Local)]]    Script Date: 2/1/2016 8:59:08 AM ******/
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT name FROM msdb.dbo.syscategories WHERE name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]' AND category_class=1)
BEGIN
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_category @class=N'JOB', @type=N'LOCAL', @name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback

END

DECLARE @jobId BINARY(16)
EXEC @ReturnCode =  msdb.dbo.sp_add_job @job_name=N'Re-create Ax2012SlupskTEST snapshot', 
              @enabled=1, 
              @notify_level_eventlog=0, 
              @notify_level_email=0, 
              @notify_level_netsend=0, 
              @notify_level_page=0, 
              @delete_level=0, 
              @description=N'No description available.', 
              @category_name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]', 
              @owner_login_name=N'sa', @job_id = @jobId OUTPUT
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Kill sessions using snapshot]    Script Date: 2/1/2016 8:59:08 AM ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Kill sessions using snapshot', 
              @step_id=1, 
              @cmdexec_success_code=0, 
              @on_success_action=3, 
              @on_success_step_id=0, 
              @on_fail_action=2, 
              @on_fail_step_id=0, 
              @retry_attempts=0, 
              @retry_interval=0, 
              @os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
              @command=N'DECLARE @killsessions nvarchar(1024)
SET @killsessions = ''''
SELECT @killsessions = @killsessions + ''Kill '' + rtrim(CAST(spid AS NVARCHAR(max))) + ''; '' FROM sys.sysprocesses where dbid = db_id(''Ax2012SlupskTESTSS'')
IF len(@killsessions) > 0
BEGIN
       SELECT @killsessions = substring(@killsessions,1,LEN(@killsessions)-1)
       EXEC (@killsessions)
END', 
              @database_name=N'master', 
              @flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Drop snapshot]    Script Date: 2/1/2016 8:59:08 AM ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Drop snapshot', 
              @step_id=2, 
              @cmdexec_success_code=0, 
              @on_success_action=3, 
              @on_success_step_id=0, 
              @on_fail_action=2, 
              @on_fail_step_id=0, 
              @retry_attempts=0, 
              @retry_interval=0, 
              @os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
              @command=N'IF EXISTS(SELECT * FROM sys.databases WHERE source_database_id = (SELECT database_id FROM sys.databases WHERE name = ''Ax2012SlupskTEST''))
       DROP DATABASE [Ax2012SlupskTESTSS]', 
              @database_name=N'master', 
              @flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Remove file if exists]    Script Date: 2/1/2016 8:59:08 AM ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Remove file if exists', 
              @step_id=3, 
              @cmdexec_success_code=0, 
              @on_success_action=3, 
              @on_success_step_id=0, 
              @on_fail_action=2, 
              @on_fail_step_id=0, 
              @retry_attempts=0, 
              @retry_interval=0, 
              @os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'PowerShell', 
              @command=N'If (Test-Path "W:\SQLDBA\Ax2012SlupskTESTSS.ss"){
       Remove-Item "W:\SQLDBA\Ax2012SlupskTESTSS.ss"
}', 
              @database_name=N'master', 
              @flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Create snapshot]    Script Date: 2/1/2016 8:59:08 AM ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Create snapshot', 
              @step_id=4, 
              @cmdexec_success_code=0, 
              @on_success_action=1, 
              @on_success_step_id=0, 
              @on_fail_action=2, 
              @on_fail_step_id=0, 
              @retry_attempts=0, 
              @retry_interval=0, 
              @os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
              @command=N'CREATE DATABASE [Ax2012SlupskTESTSS] ON (Name = [Ax2012SlupskTEST], FILENAME = ''W:\SQLDBA\Ax2012SlupskTESTSS.ss'') AS SNAPSHOT OF Ax2012SlupskTEST

', 
              @database_name=N'master', 
              @flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_update_job @job_id = @jobId, @start_step_id = 1
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobschedule @job_id=@jobId, @name=N'Re-create Ax2012SlupskTEST snapshot', 
              @enabled=1, 
              @freq_type=4, 
              @freq_interval=1, 
              @freq_subday_type=4, 
              @freq_subday_interval=30, 
              @freq_relative_interval=0, 
              @freq_recurrence_factor=0, 
              @active_start_date=20151201, 
              @active_end_date=99991231, 
              @active_start_time=0, 
              @active_end_time=235959
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobserver @job_id = @jobId, @server_name = N'(local)'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
COMMIT TRANSACTION
GOTO EndSave
QuitWithRollback:
    IF (@@TRANCOUNT > 0) ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
EndSave:

GO






Extended events
Quick way to start an extended events session looking for certain sql text
CREATE EVENT SESSION [Search_for_sql_text] ON SERVER 

ADD EVENT sqlserver.rpc_completed(
    ACTION(sqlserver.client_hostname,sqlserver.database_name,sqlserver.sql_text)
    WHERE ([sqlserver].[like_i_sql_unicode_string]([sqlserver].[sql_text],N'SQL TEXT TO SEARCH FOR'))),

ADD EVENT sqlserver.sp_statement_completed(
    ACTION(sqlserver.client_hostname,sqlserver.database_name,sqlserver.sql_text)
    WHERE ([sqlserver].[like_i_sql_unicode_string]([sqlserver].[sql_text],N'SQL TEXT TO SEARCH FOR'))),

ADD EVENT sqlserver.sql_batch_completed(
    ACTION(sqlserver.client_hostname,sqlserver.database_name,sqlserver.sql_text)
    WHERE ([sqlserver].[like_i_sql_unicode_string]([sqlserver].[sql_text],N'SQL TEXT TO SEARCH FOR'))),

ADD EVENT sqlserver.sql_statement_completed(
    ACTION(sqlserver.client_hostname,sqlserver.database_name,sqlserver.sql_text)
    WHERE ([sqlserver].[like_i_sql_unicode_string]([sqlserver].[sql_text],N'%SQL TEXT TO SEARCH FOR%')))

ADD TARGET package0.ring_buffer
WITH (STARTUP_STATE=OFF)
GO

Kör följande för att ta fram de tabeller med flest rader och kör resultsetet.
SELECT TOP 10 'DBCC SHOWCONTIG(' + CAST(id AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ')' + CHAR(10) + 'PRINT '' ''' + CHAR(10) FROM sysindexes WHERE indid = 1 or indid = 0 ORDER BY rows DESC

Eller bara för en specifik:
DBCC SHOWCONTIG ('HumanResources.Employee') 
SQL Server 2005
SELECT a.index_id, name, avg_fragmentation_in_percent
FROM sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats (DB_ID(), NULL, NULL, NULL, 'DETAILED') AS a
JOIN sys.indexes AS b ON a.object_id = b.object_id AND a.index_id = b.index_id
order by avg_fragmentation_in_percent desc


SELECT object_id, index_id, avg_fragmentation_in_percent, page_count 
FROM sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats(DB_ID('AdventureWorks'), OBJECT_ID('HumanResources.Employee'), NULL, NULL, NULL);

SELECT * 
FROM sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats(DB_ID('AdventureWorks'), NULL, NULL, NULL , NULL);


SELECT
  db_name() AS DbName
, B.name AS TableName
, C.name AS IndexName
, C.fill_factor AS IndexFillFactor
, D.rows AS RowsCount
, A.avg_fragmentation_in_percent
, A.page_count
, GetDate() as [TimeStamp]
FROM sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats(DB_ID(),object_id('driverlogs'),NULL,NULL,NULL) A
INNER JOIN sys.objects B
   ON A.object_id = B.object_id
INNER JOIN sys.indexes C
   ON B.object_id = C.object_id AND A.index_id = C.index_id
INNER JOIN sys.partitions D
   ON B.object_id = D.object_id AND A.index_id = D.index_id
WHERE C.index_id > 0




En bättre variant från Tomas:

select top 30 t.name, i.name, convert (decimal (18, 2), f.frag) as frag, page_count,
    'alter index ' + i.name + ' on ' + t.name + ' rebuild with (online = on, maxdop = 1, fillfactor = 90);'
from (
    select object_id,
       index_id,
       convert (decimal (18, 2), avg_fragmentation_in_percent) as frag,
       page_count
    from sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats (db_id (db_name ()), null, null, null, 'LIMITED')
    where index_id > 0
    and avg_fragmentation_in_percent > 20
) f
join sys.tables t on t.object_id = f.object_id join sys.indexes i on i.object_id = f.object_id and i.index_id = f.index_id order by page_count desc, frag desc

Kolla efter index med mer än 30% fragmentering och med mer än 100 sidor.



Uppdatera index statistik för alla databaser

DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(1000)  DECLARE @DB sysname  DECLARE curDB CURSOR FORWARD_ONLY STATIC FOR     SELECT [name]     FROM master..sysdatabases    WHERE [name] NOT IN ('model', 'tempdb')    ORDER BY [name]      OPEN curDB  FETCH NEXT FROM curDB INTO @DB  WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0     BEGIN         SELECT @SQL = 'USE [' + @DB +']' + CHAR(13) + 'EXEC sp_updatestats' + CHAR(13)         PRINT @SQL         FETCH NEXT FROM curDB INTO @DB     END      CLOSE curDB  DEALLOCATE curDB
Rules of Thumb – Index Fill factor
So what should your fillfactors be set to? That is a difficult question to answer. I believe that performance should be tightly monitored in a production environment and indexes should be fine tuned as performance shows that changes are necessary. The following numbers are recommended as a general starting point:
If your database is read only (low number of updates), use a fillfactor of 100%. 
If your database is write intensive (writes greatly exceed reads), use a fill factor somewhere between 50% and 70%. 
If your database is both read and write intensive, start with a fillfactor of 80% to 90%. 
As you can see, these settings depend on the use of the table. If a specific table is only used for lookups, then its clustered index can have a fillfactor of 100% even in an update intensive environment. Furthermore, for indexes that consume fewer than 100 pages, the fillfactor settings are likely to show negligible results. 
Kollar fragmentering:

-- avg_fragmentation_in_percent (sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats) / logical scan fragmentation (dbcc showcontig) < 10%
-- avg_fragment_size_in_page (sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats) / Extent Scan Fragmentation (dbcc showcontig)
-- avg_page_space_used_in_percent (sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats) / Avg. Page Density (dbcc showcontig) > 90 %


Get all user def table info with rowcount and size

CREATE TABLE #temp (TableName NVARCHAR (128), RowsCnt VARCHAR (11), ReservedSpace VARCHAR(18), DataSpace VARCHAR(18), CombinedIndexSpace VARCHAR(18), UnusedSpace VARCHAR(18))
EXEC sp_MSforeachtable 'INSERT INTO #temp (TableName, RowsCnt, ReservedSpace, DataSpace, CombinedIndexSpace, UnusedSpace) EXEC sp_spaceused ''?'', FALSE'
SELECT TableName, RowsCnt, ReservedSpace, DataSpace, CombinedIndexSpace, UnusedSpace 
FROM #temp
ORDER BY TableName
DROP TABLE #temp
Table and index sizes

WITH table_space_usage
( schema_name, table_name, index_name, used, reserved, ind_rows, tbl_rows )
AS (
SELECT s.Name
     , o.Name
     , coalesce(i.Name, 'HEAP')
     , p.used_page_count * 8
     , p.reserved_page_count * 8
     , p.row_count
     , case when i.index_id in ( 0, 1 ) then p.row_count else 0 end
  FROM sys.dm_db_partition_stats p
  INNER JOIN sys.objects as o
    ON o.object_id = p.object_id
  INNER JOIN sys.schemas as s
    ON s.schema_id = o.schema_id
  LEFT OUTER JOIN sys.indexes as i
    on i.object_id = p.object_id and i.index_id = p.index_id
 WHERE o.type_desc = 'USER_TABLE'
   and o.is_ms_shipped = 0
)
 SELECT t.schema_name
     , t.table_name
     , t.index_name
     , sum(t.used) as used_in_kb
     , sum(t.reserved) as reserved_in_kb
     , case grouping(t.index_name) 
	when 0 then sum(t.ind_rows) 
	else sum(t.tbl_rows) end as rows
 FROM table_space_usage as t
 GROUP BY
       t.schema_name
     , t.table_name
     , t.index_name
 WITH ROLLUP
 ORDER BY
      grouping(t.schema_name)
    , t.schema_name
    , grouping(t.table_name)
    , t.table_name
    , grouping(t.index_name)
    , t.index_name

EXEC sp_spaceused-- Table row counts and sizes.

SELECT SUM(CAST([rows] AS int)) AS [rows]FROM   #t DROP TABLE #t

Exempel 2

DECLARE @sql nvarchar(MAX)

SELECT
    @sql = COALESCE(@sql + ' UNION ALL ', '') +
        'SELECT
            ''' + s.name + ''' AS ''Schema'',
            ''' + t.name + ''' AS ''Table'',
            COUNT(*) AS Count
            FROM ' + QUOTENAME(s.name) + '.' + QUOTENAME(t.name)
    FROM sys.schemas s
    INNER JOIN sys.tables t ON t.schema_id = s.schema_id
	WHERE t.name like 'monitor_%'
    ORDER BY
        s.name,
        t.name
--PRINT @sql
EXEC(@sql)

Exempel 3

CREATE TABLE #counts
(
    table_name varchar(255),
    row_count int
)
EXEC sp_MSForEachTable @command1='INSERT #counts (table_name, row_count) SELECT ''?'', COUNT(*) FROM ?'
SELECT table_name, row_count FROM #counts ORDER BY table_name, row_count DESC

--Get all the monitor_ tables
SELECT SUM(row_count) AS total_row_count FROM #counts WHERE table_name like '%monitor_%'
DROP TABLE #counts
Database sizes
Databas och loggstorlek i MB

select d.name
,convert(decimal(18,2),(sum(size)*8)/1024.0) as databas_storlek_mb
,(select (size*8)/1024.0 from sys.sysaltfiles where dbid=saf.dbid and groupid=0) as logg_storlek_mb
from sys.sysaltfiles saf
join sys.databases d on saf.dbid=d.database_id
where groupid>0 
group by dbid,d.name,d.compatibility_level,d.recovery_model_desc

Index size
SELECT i.[name] AS IndexName
    ,SUM(s.[used_page_count]) * 8 / 1024 AS IndexSizeMB
FROM sys.dm_db_partition_stats AS s
INNER JOIN sys.indexes AS i ON s.[object_id] = i.[object_id]
    AND s.[index_id] = i.[index_id]
GROUP BY i.[name]
ORDER BY IndexSizeMB DESC


Använt och ledigt utrymme I datafilerna
select  SUM(convert(decimal(12,2),round(a.size/128.000,2))) as FILE_SIZE_MB,
		SUM(convert(decimal(12,2),round(fileproperty(a.name,'SpaceUsed')/128.000,2))) as SPACE_USED_MB,
		SUM(convert(decimal(12,2),round((a.size-fileproperty(a.name,'SpaceUsed'))/128.000,2))) as FREE_SPACE_MB
		,a.type_desc
from	sys.master_files a
group by a.type_desc


select  a.physical_name
		,convert(decimal(12,2),round(a.size/128.000,2)) as FILE_SIZE_MB,
		convert(decimal(12,2),round(fileproperty(a.name,'SpaceUsed')/128.000,2)) as SPACE_USED_MB,
		convert(decimal(12,2),round((a.size-fileproperty(a.name,'SpaceUsed'))/128.000,2)) as FREE_SPACE_MB
		,a.type_desc
from	sys.master_files a
order by 1 desc

Allokerat och använt utrymme för alla databaser
CREATE TABLE #FileSize
(dbName NVARCHAR(128), 
    FileName NVARCHAR(128), 
    type_desc NVARCHAR(128),
    CurrentSizeMB DECIMAL(10,2), 
    FreeSpaceMB DECIMAL(10,2)
);
    
INSERT INTO #FileSize(dbName, FileName, type_desc, CurrentSizeMB, FreeSpaceMB)
exec sp_msforeachdb 
'use [?]; 
 SELECT DB_NAME() AS DbName, 
        name AS FileName, 
        type_desc,
        size/128.0 AS CurrentSizeMB,  
        size/128.0 - CAST(FILEPROPERTY(name, ''SpaceUsed'') AS INT)/128.0 AS FreeSpaceMB
FROM sys.database_files
WHERE type IN (0,1);';
    
SELECT * 
FROM #FileSize
WHERE dbName NOT IN ('distribution', 'master', 'model', 'msdb')
--AND FreeSpaceMB > ?;
    
DROP TABLE #FileSize;
Loggstorlek i MB och använt i %
DBCC SQLPERF(LOGSPACE)

What is holding the log from shrink
select name, log_reuse_wait_desc from sys.databases

I 8KB pages
select distinct
sysmaster.name,sysmaster.size 
from sys.databases sysdb,sys.master_files sysmaster
where sysdb.database_id = sysmaster.database_id

I MB
select distinct
sysmaster.name,(sysmaster.size/128) as MB 
from sys.databases sysdb,sys.master_files sysmaster
where sysdb.database_id = sysmaster.database_id


I MB mer utförligt och sorterat, utan snapshots
select	sysdb.name,
		sysmaster.name as logical_file_name,
		sysdb.recovery_model_desc, 
		sysmaster.physical_name, 
		CASE sysmaster.type_desc
			WHEN 'ROWS' THEN 'DATA'
			WHEN 'LOG' THEN 'LOG'
		END as filetype,
		(sysmaster.size/128) as MB
from	sys.databases sysdb,sys.master_files sysmaster
where	sysdb.database_id = sysmaster.database_id
and		sysdb.name not like '%snapshot%'
and		sysdb.name not in ('master','model','msdb','tempdb')
and		sysdb.state_desc = 'ONLINE' 
order by sysdb.name, filetype


Olika ytterligare exempel

Summerat per filtyp (LOG/DATA) och en DB, storlek anger filstorlek

select	sysdb.name,
		CASE sysmaster.type_desc
			WHEN 'ROWS' THEN 'DATA'
			WHEN 'LOG' THEN 'LOG'
		END as filetype,
		sum((sysmaster.size/128)) as MB
from	sys.databases sysdb
join	sys.master_files sysmaster on sysdb.database_id = sysmaster.database_id
where	sysdb.database_id = db_id(‘databasnamn’)
and		sysdb.name not like '%snapshot%'
group by sysmaster.type_desc, sysdb.name


select	sysdb.name,
		CASE sysmaster.type_desc
			WHEN 'ROWS' THEN 'DATA'
			WHEN 'LOG' THEN 'LOG'
		END as filetype,
		sum((sysmaster.size/128)) as MB
from	sys.databases sysdb
join	sys.master_files sysmaster on sysdb.database_id = sysmaster.database_id
--where	sysdb.database_id = db_id()
where		sysdb.name not like '%snapshot%'
and 		sysdb.name not in ('tempdb','master','model','msdb')
group by sysdb.name, sysmaster.type_desc




select	sysdb.name, 
		--sysdb.recovery_model_desc, 
		sysmaster.physical_name, 
		CASE sysmaster.type_desc
			WHEN 'ROWS' THEN 'DATA'
			WHEN 'LOG' THEN 'LOG'
		END as filetype,
		(sysmaster.size/128) as MB
		--sum(sysmaster.size/128) as MB
from	sys.databases sysdb,sys.master_files sysmaster
where	sysdb.database_id = sysmaster.database_id
--and		upper(sysmaster.physical_name) like 'D:%'
order by substring(sysmaster.physical_name, 1,2), MB desc
--order by sysdb.name, filetype

Virtuella loggfiler i loggfilen, kolla om den går att shrinka
-- Visar VLF'er i loggfilen:
-- Fileid, FileSize, StartOffset, FSeqNo, Status, Parity, CreateLSN
-- Om det längst ner står en tvåa betyderdet att den används i slutet av filen och kan inte shrinkas
-- Ta loggbackuper och se om den får bort det från slutet, testa shrink då.

Create Table #stage(
	RecoveryUnitId int
  , FileID      int
  , FileSize    bigint
  , StartOffset bigint
  , FSeqNo      bigint
  , [Status]    bigint
  , Parity      bigint
  , CreateLSN   numeric(38)
);
 
Create Table #results(
    Database_Name   sysname
  , VLF_count       int 
);
 
Exec sp_msforeachdb N'Use ?; 
            Insert Into #stage 
            Exec sp_executeSQL N''DBCC LogInfo(?)''; 
 
            Insert Into #results 
            Select DB_Name(), Count(*) 
            From #stage; 
 
            Truncate Table #stage;'
 
Select * 
From #results
Order By VLF_count Desc;
 
Drop Table #stage;
Drop Table #results;
 
Shrink logfile

BACKUP LOG TestDB TO DISK='C:\TestDB1.bak'
DBCC SHRINKFILE (<FileName>, <TargetSize>)

Filename är filen som ska tas ner i storlek.
Targetsize är önskade storleken i MB. Heltal enbart.
Går den inte ner i storlek tillräckligt kan man ta ytterligare backup och testa att shrinka igen.

Add log file
ALTER DATABASE [database_name] ADD LOG FILE ( NAME = N'database_nameExtraLog', FILENAME = N'N:\ database_nameExtraLog.ldf' , SIZE = 20480KB , FILEGROWTH = 1024KB )
Exemple of removing extra log file
CREATE DATABASE rDb ON  PRIMARY   ( NAME = N'rDb', FILENAME = N'C:\rDb.mdf' , SIZE = 50MB , FILEGROWTH = 1024KB ) LOG ON   (NAME = N'rDb_log2', FILENAME = N'C:\rDb_log2.ldf', SIZE = 3MB, FILEGROWTH = 2MB) ,(NAME = N'rDb_log3', FILENAME = N'C:\rDb_log3.ldf', SIZE = 3MB, FILEGROWTH = 2MB) ,(NAME = N'rDb_log4', FILENAME = N'C:\rDb_log4.ldf', SIZE = 3MB, FILEGROWTH = 2MB) GO ALTER DATABASE rDb SET RECOVERY FULL BACKUP DATABASE rDb TO DISK = 'C:\rDb.bak' WITH INIT CREATE TABLE rDb..t(c1 INT IDENTITY, c2 CHAR(100)) INSERT INTO rDb..t SELECT TOP(15000) 'hello' FROM syscolumns AS a   CROSS JOIN syscolumns AS b --Log is now about 46% full DBCC SQLPERF(logspace) --Check virtual log file layout DBCC LOGINFO(rDb) --See that file 4 isn't used at all (Status = 0 for all 4's rows) --We can remove file 4, it isn't used ALTER DATABASE rDb REMOVE FILE rDb_log4 --Check virtual log file layout DBCC LOGINFO(rDb) --Can't remove 3 since it is in use ALTER DATABASE rDb REMOVE FILE rDb_log3 --What if we backup log? BACKUP LOG rDb TO DISK = 'C:\rDb.bak' --Check virtual log file layout DBCC LOGINFO(rDb) --3 is still in use (status = 2) --Can't remove 3 since it is in use ALTER DATABASE rDb REMOVE FILE rDb_log3 --Shrink 3 USE rDb DBCC SHRINKFILE(rDb_log3) USE master --... and backup log? BACKUP LOG rDb TO DISK = 'C:\rDb.bak' --Check virtual log file layout DBCC LOGINFO(rDb) --3 is no longer in use --Can now remove 3 since it is not in use ALTER DATABASE rDb REMOVE FILE rDb_log3 --Check explorer, we're down to 1 log file --See what sys.database_files say? SELECT * FROM rDb.sys.database_files --Seems physical file is gone, but SQL Server consider the file offline --Backup log does it: BACKUP LOG rDb TO DISK = 'C:\rDb.bak' SELECT * FROM rDb.sys.database_files --Can never remove the first ("primary") log file ALTER DATABASE rDb REMOVE FILE rDb_log2 --Note error message from above



Is a database being used?
Check with index stats
WITH LastActivity (ObjectID, LastAction) AS 
  (
       SELECT object_id AS TableName,
              last_user_seek as LastAction
         FROM sys.dm_db_index_usage_stats u
        WHERE database_id = db_id(db_name())
        UNION 
       SELECT object_id AS TableName,
              last_user_scan as LastAction
         FROM sys.dm_db_index_usage_stats u
        WHERE database_id = db_id(db_name())
        UNION
       SELECT object_id AS TableName,
              last_user_lookup as LastAction
         FROM sys.dm_db_index_usage_stats u
        WHERE database_id = db_id(db_name())
  )
  SELECT OBJECT_NAME(so.object_id) AS TableName,
         MAX(la.LastAction) as LastSelect
    FROM sys.objects so
    LEFT
    JOIN LastActivity la
      on so.object_id = la.ObjectID
   WHERE so.type = 'U'
     AND so.object_id > 100
GROUP BY OBJECT_NAME(so.object_id)
ORDER BY OBJECT_NAME(so.object_id)


Processes or sql plans being updated
SELECT @@ServerName AS server
 ,NAME AS dbname
 ,COUNT(STATUS) AS number_of_connections
 ,GETDATE() AS timestamp
FROM sys.databases sd
LEFT JOIN sysprocesses sp ON sd.database_id = sp.dbid
WHERE database_id NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 4
GROUP BY NAME

SELECT @@ServerName AS SERVER
 ,NAME
 ,login_time
 ,last_batch
 ,getdate() AS DATE
 ,STATUS
 ,hostname
 ,program_name
 ,nt_username
 ,loginame
FROM sys.databases d
LEFT JOIN sysprocesses sp ON d.database_id = sp.dbid
WHERE database_id NOT BETWEEN 0
  AND 4
 AND loginame IS NOT NULL

SELECT *
FROM sys.dm_os_performance_counters
WHERE counter_name like 'Transactions/sec%'
and instance_name like 'MSSQL%';
 

SELECT
    SUBSTRING(tx.[text],
    (qs.statement_start_offset / 2) + 1,
    (CASE WHEN qs.statement_end_offset =-1 THEN DATALENGTH(tx.text) ELSE qs.statement_end_offset END - qs.statement_start_offset)
    / 2 + 1) AS QueryText,
    case when pl.query_plan LIKE '%<MissingIndexes>%' then 1 else 0 end as [Missing Indexes?],
    qs.execution_count,
    qs.total_worker_time/execution_count AS avg_cpu_time,
    qs.total_worker_time AS total_cpu_time,
    qs.total_logical_reads/execution_count AS avg_logical_reads,
    qs.total_logical_reads,
    qs.creation_time AS [plan creation time],
    qs.last_execution_time [last execution time],
    CAST(pl.query_plan AS XML) AS sqlplan
FROM    sys.dm_exec_query_stats AS qs
        CROSS APPLY sys.dm_exec_text_query_plan(qs.plan_handle, qs.statement_start_offset, qs.statement_end_offset) AS pl
        CROSS APPLY sys.dm_exec_sql_text(qs.sql_handle) AS tx
WHERE pl.query_plan LIKE '%[DATABASENAME YOUR LOOKING FOR]%'
ORDER BY execution_count DESC OPTION (RECOMPILE);


Last login time per login
SELECT DB_NAME(r.database_id),max(login_time),login_name,max(last_request_end_time)
FROM sys.dm_exec_requests AS R JOIN sys.dm_exec_sessions S ON R.session_id=S.session_id
         CROSS APPLY sys.dm_exec_sql_text(sql_handle) AS ST
WHERE plan_handle IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY  DB_NAME(r.database_id),login_name


Compress table and indexes
A Brute Force Way to Compress a Database, SQL 2008 E och Dev only

CREATE PROCEDURE dbo.sp_dba_compress_user_objects @compression_type nvarchar(20) = 'PAGE', @minutes_to_run int = 1440

AS

DECLARE @schema_name        sysname,
        @object_name        sysname,
        @sql_string         nvarchar(max),
        @row_count          int,
        @start_time         datetime,
        @index_type_desc    nvarchar(60)
       
SELECT  @row_count          = 1,
        @start_time         = GETDATE(),
        @compression_type   = UPPER(@compression_type)

IF @compression_type NOT IN ('ROW', 'PAGE')
    RAISERROR('@compression_type must be ''ROW'' or ''PAGE''', 16, 1)

WHILE @row_count > 0 AND DATEADD(mi, @minutes_to_run, @start_time) > GETDATE()
 BEGIN
    SELECT      TOP 1
                @schema_name = OBJECT_SCHEMA_NAME(p.object_id, DB_ID()),
                @object_name = OBJECT_NAME(p.object_id, DB_ID()),
                @index_type_desc = ips.index_type_desc
    FROM        sys.partitions p
                INNER JOIN sys.dm_db_index_physical_stats(DB_ID(), NULL, NULL, NULL, 'LIMITED') ips
                    ON p.object_id = ips.object_id
                        AND p.index_id = ips.index_id
                LEFT OUTER JOIN sys.columns c
                    ON p.object_id = c.object_id
                        AND c.is_sparse = 1
    WHERE       p.data_compression_desc = 'NONE'
                    AND OBJECTPROPERTY(p.object_id, 'IsUserTable') = 1
                        AND OBJECTPROPERTY(c.object_id, 'IsMSShipped') = 0
                            AND c.object_id IS NULL
    ORDER BY    ips.page_count

    SELECT  @row_count = @@ROWCOUNT
   
    IF @row_count > 0
     BEGIN
        IF @index_type_desc = 'HEAP'
         BEGIN
            SELECT  @sql_string = 'ALTER TABLE ' + QUOTENAME(@schema_name) + '.' + QUOTENAME(@object_name) + ' REBUILD PARTITION=ALL WITH (DATA_COMPRESSION=' + @compression_type + ', ONLINE=ON)'
            RAISERROR(@sql_string, 0, 0) WITH NOWAIT
            EXEC    sp_executesql @sql_string
         END
        SELECT  @sql_string = 'ALTER INDEX ALL ON ' + QUOTENAME(@schema_name) + '.' + QUOTENAME(@object_name) + ' REBUILD PARTITION=ALL WITH (DATA_COMPRESSION=' + @compression_type + ', ONLINE=ON)'
        RAISERROR(@sql_string, 0, 0) WITH NOWAIT
        EXEC    sp_executesql @sql_string
     END
    ELSE
     BEGIN
        PRINT 'No work to do.'
     END
 END
 
 IF GETDATE() > DATEADD(mi, @minutes_to_run, @start_time)
  BEGIN
    PRINT 'Shut down to due long run time.'
  END
GO
Check failed jobs

-- CREATE PROCEDURE 

CREATE PROC check_failed_jobs @NumDays int
AS

SET NOCOUNT ON
PRINT 	'Checking for all jobs that have failed in the last ' + CAST(@NumDays AS char(2)) +' days.......'
PRINT	' '

SELECT 	CAST(CONVERT(datetime,CAST(run_date AS char(8)),101) AS char(11))	AS 'Failure Date',
	SUBSTRING(T2.name,1,40)							AS 'Job Name',
	T1.step_id 								AS 'Step #',
	T1.step_name								AS 'Step Name',
	T1.message								AS 'Message'

FROM	msdb..sysjobhistory 	T1
JOIN	msdb..sysjobs		T2
	ON T1.job_id = T2.job_id

WHERE		T1.run_status != 1
	AND	T1.step_id != 0
	AND	run_date >= CONVERT(char(8), (select dateadd (day,(-1*@NumDays), getdate())), 112)

GO

-- EXECUTE PROC and PASS NUMBER OF DAYS TO CHECK FOR
--   JOB FAILURES - example uses 2 days

check_failed_jobs 2

Find Largest size Tables in a Database

IF EXISTS 
    (
	SELECT 1 FROM master.dbo.sysobjects 
	WHERE name = 'sp_LargestTables' AND type = 'P'
    )
DROP PROC sp_LargestTables
GO

CREATE PROC sp_LargestTables(@n int = NULL,@IsSystemAllowed bit = 0)
AS
/*=========================================================================
CREATE DATE		:	Hari N Sharma
CREATION DATE		:	10-09-2007
LAST MODIFICATION DATE	:	11-10-2007

PURPOSE	: To get a list of User/System tables according to their size.
=========================================================================*/

BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON
	DECLARE @LOW int
	SELECT @LOW = LOW FROM master.dbo.spt_values (NOLOCK) WHERE number = 1 AND type = 'E' 

	IF @n > 0 SET ROWCOUNT @n

	SELECT TableName,[Row Count],[Size (KB)] FROM 
	(
		SELECT QUOTENAME(USER_NAME(o.uid)) + '.' + QUOTENAME(OBJECT_NAME(i.id)) AS TableName,SUM(i.rowcnt) [Row Count],
		CONVERT(numeric(15,2),(((CONVERT(numeric(15,2),SUM(i.reserved)) * @LOW) / 1024.0))) AS [Size (KB)]
		FROM sysindexes i INNER JOIN sysobjects o (NOLOCK) ON i.id = o.id AND 
		((@IsSystemAllowed = 1 AND o.type IN ('U', 'S')) OR o.type = 'U') 
		WHERE	indid IN (0, 1, 255)
		GROUP BY QUOTENAME(USER_NAME(o.uid)) + '.' + QUOTENAME(OBJECT_NAME(i.id))
	) AS Z
	ORDER BY [Size (KB)] DESC

	SET ROWCOUNT 0
END

GO

Count records without accessing the table
CREATE PROCEDURE dbo.procSample
 AS
   SET NOCOUNT ON
   DECLARE @recCount int
   SELECT   @recCount = rows
   FROM     sysindexes WITH (NOLOCK)
   WHERE    id = OBJECT_ID(‘authors’)
            AND indid < 2
RETURN @recCount





Eller bara som en select:

   SELECT   rows
   FROM     sysindexes WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE    id = OBJECT_ID(‘tablename’)
            AND indid < 2

Hitta stora tabeller via primary key
SELECT   name, rowcnt
FROM     sysindexes WITH (NOLOCK) 
WHERE    indid < 2
and		 name like 'PK%'
and		 rowcnt > 100000000
order by rowcnt desc

Count rows in all user tables
select    [Owner] = left(user_name(objectproperty(id, 'OwnerId')), 35)
        , [Table] = left(object_name(id), 65)
        , [Count] = rows
from dbo.sysindexes
where indid                             < 2
  and objectproperty(id, 'IsUserTable') = 1
order by 1,2


Memory
Se  HYPERLINK "http://blogs.msdn.com/psssql/archive/2009/09/11/fun-with-locked-pages-awe-task-manager-and-the-working-set.aspx" http://blogs.msdn.com/psssql/archive/2009/09/11/fun-with-locked-pages-awe-task-manager-and-the-working-set.aspx för en utförlig artikel om sql server minne
The amount of physical memory supported has increased with the introduction of the Windows Server 2003 family. The physical memory accessible by AWE depends on which operating system you are using. The following list provides the maximum physical memory accessible by each Windows Server 2003 operating system at the time of writing.
Windows Server 2003, Standard Edition supports physical memory up to 4 GB.
Windows Server 2003, Enterprise Edition supports physical memory up to 32 GB.
Windows Server 2003, Datacenter Edition supports physical memory up to 64 GB.
Kolla hur mycket minne en 2008 instans använder
select * from sys.dm_os_process_memory

SQL Server 2005 bör ha locked pages in memory.
To enable the lock pages in memory option 
On the Start menu, click Run. In the Open box, type gpedit.msc. 
The Group Policy dialog box opens. 
On the Group Policy console, expand Computer Configuration, and then expand Windows Settings.
Expand Security Settings, and then expand Local Policies.
Select the User Rights Assignment folder. 
The policies will be displayed in the details pane. 
In the pane, double-click Lock pages in memory.
In the Local Security Policy Setting dialog box, click Add.
In the Select Users or Groups dialog box, add an account with privileges to run sqlservr.exe.
AWE
AWE is for 32 bit machines only to overcome the 4GB limit.
AWE is not needed and cannot be configured on 64-bit operating systems. But Lock pages in memory on 64bit systems should be used anyway.
To make AWE available to an instance of SQL Server 2005, use sp_configure to set the awe enabled option to 1, and then restart SQL Server. 
For more information about min server memory and max server memory, see  HYPERLINK "http://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/ms178067%28SQL.90%29.aspx" Server Memory Options.
Before enabling AWE, you must configure the Lock Pages in Memory policy. See above.

How long has SQL Server been running?
select 'Sql Server Service has been running for about '
+ cast((datediff(hh, create_date, getdate()))/24 as varchar(3)) + ' days and '
+ cast((datediff(hh, create_date, getdate())) % 24 as varchar(2)) + ' hours'
from sys.databases where name = 'tempdb'


Jobb
Kolla backupjobb en vecka bakåt
SELECT  
   CONVERT(CHAR(100), SERVERPROPERTY('Servername')) AS Server, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name,  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_start_date,  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.expiration_date, 
   CASE msdb..backupset.type  
       WHEN 'D' THEN 'Database'  
       WHEN 'L' THEN 'Log'  
       WHEN 'I' THEN 'Diff'  
   END AS backup_type,  
   CASE msdb.dbo.backupset.is_copy_only
	WHEN 0 THEN 'No'
	WHEN 1 THEN 'Yes'
   END AS is_copyonly,
   ceiling(msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_size /1048576) as 'Size Meg',
   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.logical_device_name,  
   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.physical_device_name,   
   msdb.dbo.backupset.name AS backupset_name, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.description
FROM   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily  
   INNER JOIN msdb.dbo.backupset ON msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.media_set_id = msdb.dbo.backupset.media_set_id  
WHERE  (CONVERT(datetime, msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_start_date, 102) >= GETDATE() - 7)  
and msdb.dbo.backupset.is_copy_only = 1
ORDER BY  	
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date desc

Kolla backupfilnamn för en specifik backup
SELECT  TOP 1
   CONVERT(CHAR(100), SERVERPROPERTY('Servername')) AS Server, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name,  
   (msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date) AS last_db_backup_date,
   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.physical_device_name
FROM   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily  
   INNER JOIN msdb.dbo.backupset ON msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.media_set_id = msdb.dbo.backupset.media_set_id  
WHERE  msdb.dbo.backupset.type = 'D' 
AND	   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name = 'OPTAC_Localadmin'
ORDER BY  
msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date DESC   


Kolla så att backuper finns på disk
SET NOCOUNT ON 
DECLARE @FileName VARCHAR(255) 
DECLARE @File_Exists INT 
DECLARE @DBname sysname 

--get list of files to check 
DECLARE FileNameCsr CURSOR 
READ_ONLY 
FOR  
   SELECT physical_device_name, sd.name 
   FROM msdb..backupmediafamily bmf 
   INNER JOIN msdb..backupset bms ON bmf.media_set_id = bms.media_set_id 
   INNER JOIN master..sysdatabases sd ON bms.database_name = sd.name 
   AND bms.backup_start_date = (SELECT MAX(backup_start_date) FROM [msdb]..[backupset] b2 
                                   WHERE bms.database_name = b2.database_name AND b2.type = 'D') 
   WHERE sd.name NOT IN ('Pubs','tempdb','Northwind', 'Adventureworks') 

BEGIN TRY 
   OPEN FileNameCsr 

   FETCH NEXT FROM FileNameCsr INTO @FileName, @DBname 
   WHILE (@@fetch_status <> -1) 
   BEGIN 
       IF (@@fetch_status <> -2) 
       BEGIN 
           EXEC Master.dbo.xp_fileexist @FileName, @File_Exists OUT 
        
           --if the file is not found, print out a message 
           IF @File_Exists = 0 --0 means file is not found, 1 means it is found 
               PRINT 'File Not Found: ' + @FileName + ' -- for database: ' + @DBName 
       END 
    
   FETCH NEXT FROM FileNameCsr INTO @FileName, @DBName 
   END 
    
END TRY 

BEGIN CATCH 
    SELECT 
        ERROR_NUMBER() AS ErrorNumber 
        ,ERROR_SEVERITY() AS ErrorSeverity 
        ,ERROR_STATE() AS ErrorState 
        ,ERROR_PROCEDURE() AS ErrorProcedure 
        ,ERROR_LINE() AS ErrorLine 
        ,ERROR_MESSAGE() AS ErrorMessage; 
END CATCH 


CLOSE FileNameCsr 
DEALLOCATE FileNameCsr 
GO


Se senaste backupjobb

SELECT     CONVERT(CHAR(100), SERVERPROPERTY('Servername')) AS Server,    msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name,     MAX(msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date) AS last_db_backup_date FROM   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily     INNER JOIN msdb.dbo.backupset ON msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.media_set_id = msdb.dbo.backupset.media_set_id  WHERE  msdb..backupset.type = 'D' GROUP BY    msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name  ORDER BY     msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name

Backuper ordnade per datum

Select a.name,Backup_Date from master.dbo.sysdatabases a
	left join 
            (select database_name,max(backup_finish_date) backup_date
            from msdb.dbo.backupset where backup_finish_date <= getdate()
            group by database_name)  B
	on  a.name = b.database_name
order by backup_date desc

Alla databasers backuper de senaste XX dagarna

SELECT  
   CONVERT(CHAR(100), SERVERPROPERTY('Servername')) AS Server, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name,  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_start_date,  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.expiration_date, 
   CASE msdb..backupset.type  
       WHEN 'D' THEN 'Database'  
       WHEN 'L' THEN 'Log'  
   END AS backup_type,  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_size,  
   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.logical_device_name,  
   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.physical_device_name,   
   msdb.dbo.backupset.name AS backupset_name, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.description 
FROM   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily  
   INNER JOIN msdb.dbo.backupset ON msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.media_set_id = msdb.dbo.backupset.media_set_id  
WHERE  (CONVERT(datetime, msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_start_date, 102) >= GETDATE() - 7)  
ORDER BY  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date

Senaste backupen per databas

SELECT  
   CONVERT(CHAR(100), SERVERPROPERTY('Servername')) AS Server, 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name,  
   MAX(msdb.dbo.backupset.backup_finish_date) AS last_db_backup_date 
FROM   msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily  
   INNER JOIN msdb.dbo.backupset ON msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily.media_set_id = msdb.dbo.backupset.media_set_id  
WHERE  msdb..backupset.type = 'D' 
GROUP BY 
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name  
ORDER BY  
   msdb.dbo.backupset.database_name

Backup type, msdb..backupset.type kan vara:
D = Database 
I = Differential database 
L = Log 
F = File or filegroup 
G =Differential file 
P = Partial 
Q = Differential partial 
Can be NULL.

Schema över alla jobb

Skapar en funktion för att snygga till utskriften ordentligt

USE msdb
GO

--drop function [dbo].[udf_schedule_description]

CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[udf_schedule_description] (@freq_type INT ,
  @freq_interval INT ,
  @freq_subday_type INT ,
  @freq_subday_interval INT ,
  @freq_relative_interval INT ,
  @freq_recurrence_factor INT ,
  @active_start_date INT ,
  @active_end_date INT,
  @active_start_time INT ,
  @active_end_time INT )
RETURNS NVARCHAR(255) AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @schedule_description NVARCHAR(255)
DECLARE @loop INT
DECLARE @idle_cpu_percent INT
DECLARE @idle_cpu_duration INT

IF (@freq_type = 0x1) -- OneTime
BEGIN
SELECT @schedule_description = N'Once on ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @active_start_date) + N' at ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, cast((@active_start_time / 10000) as varchar(10)) + ':' + right('00' + cast((@active_start_time % 10000) / 100 as varchar(10)),2))
RETURN @schedule_description
END
IF (@freq_type = 0x4) -- Daily
BEGIN
SELECT @schedule_description = N'Every day '
END
IF (@freq_type = 0x8) -- Weekly
BEGIN
SELECT @schedule_description = N'Every ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_recurrence_factor) + N' week(s) on '
SELECT @loop = 1
WHILE (@loop <= 7)
BEGIN
IF (@freq_interval & POWER(2, @loop - 1) = POWER(2, @loop - 1))
SELECT @schedule_description = @schedule_description + DATENAME(dw, N'1996120' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @loop)) + N', '
SELECT @loop = @loop + 1
END
IF (RIGHT(@schedule_description, 2) = N', ')
SELECT @schedule_description = SUBSTRING(@schedule_description, 1, (DATALENGTH(@schedule_description) / 2) - 2) + N' '
END
IF (@freq_type = 0x10) -- Monthly
BEGIN
SELECT @schedule_description = N'Every ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_recurrence_factor) + N' months(s) on day ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_interval) + N' of that month '
END
IF (@freq_type = 0x20) -- Monthly Relative
BEGIN
SELECT @schedule_description = N'Every ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_recurrence_factor) + N' months(s) on the '
SELECT @schedule_description = @schedule_description +
CASE @freq_relative_interval
WHEN 0x01 THEN N'first '
WHEN 0x02 THEN N'second '
WHEN 0x04 THEN N'third '
WHEN 0x08 THEN N'fourth '
WHEN 0x10 THEN N'last '
END +
CASE
WHEN (@freq_interval > 00)
AND (@freq_interval < 08) THEN DATENAME(dw, N'1996120' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_interval))
WHEN (@freq_interval = 08) THEN N'day'
WHEN (@freq_interval = 09) THEN N'week day'
WHEN (@freq_interval = 10) THEN N'weekend day'
END + N' of that month '
END
IF (@freq_type = 0x40) -- AutoStart
BEGIN
SELECT @schedule_description = FORMATMESSAGE(14579)
RETURN @schedule_description
END
IF (@freq_type = 0x80) -- OnIdle
BEGIN
EXECUTE master.dbo.xp_instance_regread N'HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE',
N'SOFTWARE\Microsoft\MSSQLServer\SQLServerAgent',
N'IdleCPUPercent',
@idle_cpu_percent OUTPUT,
N'no_output'
EXECUTE master.dbo.xp_instance_regread N'HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE',
N'SOFTWARE\Microsoft\MSSQLServer\SQLServerAgent',
N'IdleCPUDuration',
@idle_cpu_duration OUTPUT,
N'no_output'
SELECT @schedule_description = FORMATMESSAGE(14578, ISNULL(@idle_cpu_percent, 10), ISNULL(@idle_cpu_duration, 600))
RETURN @schedule_description
END
-- Subday stuff
SELECT @schedule_description = @schedule_description +
CASE @freq_subday_type
WHEN 0x1 THEN N'at ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, cast((@active_start_time / 10000) as varchar(10)) + ':' + right('00' + cast((@active_start_time % 10000) / 100 as varchar(10)),2))
WHEN 0x2 THEN N'every ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_subday_interval) + N' second(s)'
WHEN 0x4 THEN N'every ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_subday_interval) + N' minute(s)'
WHEN 0x8 THEN N'every ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, @freq_subday_interval) + N' hour(s)'
END
IF (@freq_subday_type IN (0x2, 0x4, 0x8))
SELECT @schedule_description = @schedule_description + N' between ' +
CONVERT(NVARCHAR, cast((@active_start_time / 10000) as varchar(10)) + ':' + right('00' + cast((@active_start_time % 10000) / 100 as varchar(10)),2) ) + N' and ' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR, cast((@active_end_time / 10000) as varchar(10)) + ':' + right('00' + cast((@active_end_time % 10000) / 100 as varchar(10)),2) )

RETURN @schedule_description
END


-- Selecta jobben med beskrinvningen från funktionen vi skapade ovan

SELECT dbo.sysjobs.name, CAST(dbo.sysschedules.active_start_time / 10000 AS VARCHAR(10))  
+ ':' + RIGHT('00' + CAST(dbo.sysschedules.active_start_time % 10000 / 100 AS VARCHAR(10)), 2) AS active_start_time,  
dbo.udf_schedule_description(dbo.sysschedules.freq_type, dbo.sysschedules.freq_interval, 
dbo.sysschedules.freq_subday_type, dbo.sysschedules.freq_subday_interval, dbo.sysschedules.freq_relative_interval, 
dbo.sysschedules.freq_recurrence_factor, dbo.sysschedules.active_start_date, dbo.sysschedules.active_end_date, 
dbo.sysschedules.active_start_time, dbo.sysschedules.active_end_time) AS ScheduleDscr, dbo.sysjobs.enabled 
FROM dbo.sysjobs INNER JOIN 
dbo.sysjobschedules ON dbo.sysjobs.job_id = dbo.sysjobschedules.job_id INNER JOIN 
dbo.sysschedules ON dbo.sysjobschedules.schedule_id = dbo.sysschedules.schedule_id 



OPTAC report SQL

use msdb 
go


select CAST 
        ( 
            CONVERT(VARCHAR, jh.run_date) 
            + ' ' + STUFF(STUFF(RIGHT('000000' 
            + CONVERT(VARCHAR,jh.run_time),6),5,0,':'),3,0,':') 
            AS DATETIME 
        ) AS Starttime,
CASE
	--hours, minutes and seconds
	WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) > 4 THEN CONVERT(VARCHAR(4),LEFT(jh.run_duration,LEN(jh.run_duration)-4)) * 3600
	+ LEFT(RIGHT(jh.run_duration,4),2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
	--minutes and seconds
	WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 4 THEN LEFT(jh.run_duration,2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
	WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 3 THEN LEFT(jh.run_duration,1) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
	ELSE --only seconds
	RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
	END as [Seconds],
	j.name as [key]
FROM msdb.dbo.sysjobhistory jh
JOIN msdb.dbo.sysjobs j ON jh.job_id = j.job_id
WHERE	jh.step_id = 0
/*
'MP_Backup_UserDB_Full'
'MP_UpdateStatistics_UserDB'
'MP_Backup_UserDB_Log'
'MP_IndexOptimize_UserDB'
'MP_IntegrityCheck_UserDB'
*/
AND		j.name IN ('MP_Backup_UserDB_Full','MP_UpdateStatistics_UserDB','MP_Backup_UserDB_Log','MP_IndexOptimize_UserDB','MP_IntegrityCheck_UserDB')
AND		(convert(datetime, convert(varchar(8), jh.run_date)) BETWEEN DATEADD(dd, -180, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, GETDATE())) AND DATEADD(dd, 0, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, GETDATE())))
ORDER BY j.name asc, jh.run_date asc 


Sök aktiva jobb och nästa körtid
select	ss.name,
		sjs.next_run_date as Next_Run_Date,
		ss.date_created,
		ss.active_start_date,
	stuff(stuff(right(10000000+sjs.next_run_time,6),5,0,':'),3,0,':')     as Tid
from		sysjobschedules sjs,
		sysschedules ss
where		sjs.schedule_id = ss.schedule_id
and		ss.enabled = 1
order by active_start_date


Sök jobbhändelser historiskt
declare @searchdate datetime;
select @searchdate = dateadd(dd, 0, datediff(dd, 0, convert (datetime, '2010-02-11 23:00:00', 120)))
select job_name, run_datetime, run_duration
from
(
    select job_name, run_datetime,
        SUBSTRING(run_duration, 1, 2) + ':' + SUBSTRING(run_duration, 3, 2) + ':' +
        SUBSTRING(run_duration, 5, 2) AS run_duration
    from
    (
        select DISTINCT
            j.name as job_name, 
            run_datetime = CONVERT(DATETIME, RTRIM(run_date)) +  
                (run_time * 9 + run_time % 10000 * 6 + run_time % 100 * 10) / 216e4,
            run_duration = RIGHT('000000' + CONVERT(varchar(6), run_duration), 6)
        from msdb..sysjobhistory h
        inner join msdb..sysjobs j
        on h.job_id = j.job_id
    ) t
) t
where dateadd(dd, 0, datediff(dd, 0, convert (datetime, run_datetime, 120))) = @searchdate
order by run_datetime




Jobbschema för viss databas
select	sj.name,
		sjs.next_run_date as Next_Run_Date,
		ss.date_created,
		ss.active_start_date,
	stuff(stuff(right(10000000+sjs.next_run_time,6),5,0,':'),3,0,':')     as Tid
from		sysjobschedules sjs,
		sysschedules ss,
		sysjobs sj
where		sjs.schedule_id = ss.schedule_id
and		sjs.job_id = sj.job_id
and		sj.enabled = 1
and		sj.name like '%databasename%'
order by sj.name


Jobbhistorik, med datum, status och tid
select j.name, dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time),
CASE
	--hours, minutes and seconds
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) > 4 THEN CONVERT(VARCHAR(4),LEFT(jh.run_duration,LEN(jh.run_duration)-4)) * 3600
			+ LEFT(RIGHT(jh.run_duration,4),2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
    --minutes and seconds
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 4 THEN LEFT(jh.run_duration,2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 3 THEN LEFT(jh.run_duration,1) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
    ELSE --only seconds    
			RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2) 
END as duration,
jh.run_status
from dbo.sysjobhistory jh
join dbo.sysjobs j on jh.job_id = j.job_id
where jh.step_id = 0
order by j.name asc, jh.run_date asc


Jobbhistorik, med datum, status och tid inom vissa tider och med stoppddatum

use msdb
go

select j.name, dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time),
CASE
	--hours, minutes and seconds
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) > 4 THEN CONVERT(VARCHAR(4),LEFT(jh.run_duration,LEN(jh.run_duration)-4)) * 3600
			+ LEFT(RIGHT(jh.run_duration,4),2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
    --minutes and seconds
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 4 THEN LEFT(jh.run_duration,2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 3 THEN LEFT(jh.run_duration,1) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2)
    ELSE --only seconds    
			RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2) 
END as duration,
CASE
	--hours, minutes and seconds
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) > 4 THEN dateadd(ss, cast(CONVERT(VARCHAR(4),LEFT(jh.run_duration,LEN(jh.run_duration)-4)) * 3600
			+ LEFT(RIGHT(jh.run_duration,4),2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2) as int), cast(dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time) as datetime))
    --minutes and seconds
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 4 THEN dateadd(ss, cast(LEFT(jh.run_duration,2) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2) as int), cast(dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time) as datetime))
    WHEN	LEN(jh.run_duration) = 3 THEN dateadd(ss, cast(LEFT(jh.run_duration,1) * 60 + RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2) as int), cast(dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time) as datetime))
    ELSE --only seconds    
			dateadd(ss, cast(RIGHT(jh.run_duration,2) as int), cast(dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time) as datetime))
END as stoptime,
jh.run_status
from dbo.sysjobhistory jh
join dbo.sysjobs j on jh.job_id = j.job_id
where jh.step_id = 0
and dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time) > DATEADD(dd,-14,GETDATE())
and dbo.agent_datetime(jh.run_date, jh.run_time) < DATEADD(dd, +1,GETDATE())
order by jh.run_date asc



Diverse sätt att omvandla run_duration

SELECT
runDate = CONVERT (DATETIME, RTRIM(run_date)),
run_duration/10000 Hours,
run_duration/100%100 Minutes,
run_duration%100 Seconds
from sysJobHistory


SELECT
runDate = CONVERT (DATETIME, RTRIM(run_date)),
CAST(run_duration/10000 as varchar)  + ':' + CAST(run_duration/100%100 as varchar) + ':' + CAST(run_duration%100 as varchar)
from sysJobHistory


Kör multipla index rebuildjobb parallellt

USE MSDB
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('msdb.dbo.Instance_Databases','U') IS NOT NULL
DROP TABLE msdb.dbo.Instance_Databases
GO
CREATE TABLE msdb.dbo.Instance_Databases
(
DatabaseName varchar(MAX),
Completed [bit] NULL,
Process_ID [int] NULL,
Start_Time [datetime] NULL,
Stop_Time [datetime] NULL
)
GO
INSERT INTO msdb.dbo.Instance_Databases
SELECT Name AS DatabaseName, NULL AS Completed, NULL AS Process_ID, NULL AS Start_Time, NULL AS Stop_Time
FROM sys.databases
WHERE database_id > 4 AND state = 0
ORDER BY Name ASC

GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.select_db','P') IS NOT NULL
DROP PROCEDURE dbo.select_db

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE DBO.SELECT_DB (@DatabaseNameOut VARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT )
AS
BEGIN
-- Soft-Lock data. Assign value to Process_ID and Start_Time
UPDATE dbtable
   SET Process_ID = @@spid
      ,Start_Time = getdate()
  FROM Instance_Databases dbtable
       JOIN (SELECT TOP 1 DatabaseName FROM Instance_Databases WITH (UPDLOCK, READPAST) 
              WHERE Completed IS NULL AND Process_ID IS NULL AND Start_Time IS NULL AND Stop_Time IS NULL
            ) batch
         ON dbtable.DatabaseName = batch.DatabaseName     
SELECT @DatabaseNameOut = DatabaseName FROM Instance_Databases WHERE Completed IS NULL AND Process_ID = @@spid
END  

GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.complete_db','P') IS NOT NULL
DROP PROCEDURE dbo.complete_db

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE DBO.COMPLETE_DB (@DatabaseNameIn VARCHAR(MAX))
AS
BEGIN
-- Set value to Completed
UPDATE Instance_Databases
  SET Completed = 1, Stop_Time = getdate()
  WHERE DatabaseName = @DatabaseNameIn AND Process_ID = @@spid      
END  

GO

Declare @databasenamein VARCHAR(MAX)
Declare @databasenameout VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
While @databasenameout IS NOT NULL
Begin
  Set @databasenameout = NULL
  exec msdb.dbo.select_db @databasenameout output
  If @databasenameout IS NULL Break
  Select @databasenameout
  EXECUTE msdb.dbo.IndexOptimize @Databases = @databasenameout, @FragmentationLow_NonLOB = 'INDEX_REBUILD_ONLINE', @FragmentationMedium_NonLOB = 'INDEX_REBUILD_ONLINE', @FragmentationHigh_NonLOB = 'INDEX_REBUILD_ONLINE', @PageCountLevel = 0
  Waitfor Delay '00:00:02'
  Set @databasenamein = @databasenameout
  exec msdb.dbo.complete_db @databasenamein
End

Disabla/enabla jobb automatiskt/AlwaysOn check


if exists (select replica_id 
       from sys.dm_hadr_availability_replica_states ars
       where ars.is_local = 1
       and ars.role_desc = 'PRIMARY')
  begin
    UPDATE MSDB.dbo.sysjobs
    SET Enabled = 1
    WHERE [Name] in (      select sj.name
                                         FROM msdb.dbo.sysjobs sj
                                         JOIN msdb.dbo.syscategories sc on sj.category_id = sc.category_id
                                         WHERE sc.name = 'KONHRI'
                                  )
  end
else
  begin
    UPDATE MSDB.dbo.sysjobs
    SET Enabled = 0
    WHERE [Name] in (      select sj.name
                                         FROM msdb.dbo.sysjobs sj
                                         JOIN msdb.dbo.syscategories sc on sj.category_id = sc.category_id
                                         WHERE sc.name = 'KONHRI'
                                  )
  end

Kör olika jobbsteg på primär eller sekundär AlwaysOn replika
Om det här är första jobbsteget så är steg2 den som köras på primären och IF FAIL steg 3 som körs på sekundären.

IF (SERVERPROPERTY('IsHadrEnabled') = 1) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT * FROM msdb.sys.dm_hadr_availability_replica_states ars WHERE ars.is_local = 1 AND ars.role_desc = 'PRIMARY' AND ars.operational_state_desc = 'ONLINE')
RAISERROR ('Running on replica', 16, 1)

Dump table to file on disk with an agent job
Import it by copying the source table structure and then run “Import Data”

USE [msdb]
GO

/****** Object:  Job [apslog]    Script Date: 2015-04-28 14:41:12 ******/
BEGIN TRANSACTION
DECLARE @ReturnCode INT
SELECT @ReturnCode = 0
/****** Object:  JobCategory [[Uncategorized (Local)]]    Script Date: 2015-04-28 14:41:12 ******/
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT name FROM msdb.dbo.syscategories WHERE name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]' AND category_class=1)
BEGIN
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_category @class=N'JOB', @type=N'LOCAL', @name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback

END

DECLARE @jobId BINARY(16)
EXEC @ReturnCode =  msdb.dbo.sp_add_job @job_name=N'apslog', 
		@enabled=1, 
		@notify_level_eventlog=0, 
		@notify_level_email=0, 
		@notify_level_netsend=0, 
		@notify_level_page=0, 
		@delete_level=0, 
		@description=N'No description available.', 
		@category_name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]', 
		@owner_login_name=N'GLOBAL\ADMKONHRI', @job_id = @jobId OUTPUT
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [export]    Script Date: 2015-04-28 14:41:12 ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'export', 
		@step_id=1, 
		@cmdexec_success_code=0, 
		@on_success_action=1, 
		@on_success_step_id=0, 
		@on_fail_action=2, 
		@on_fail_step_id=0, 
		@retry_attempts=0, 
		@retry_interval=0, 
		@os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'CmdExec', 
		@command=N'bcp konhri.dbo.apslog out Z:\DBBackup\tablebackup\apslog_$(ESCAPE_SQUOTE(STRTDT))_$(ESCAPE_SQUOTE(STRTTM)).csv -Ssesoco2070 -T -c -t, -r\r\n', 
		@flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_update_job @job_id = @jobId, @start_step_id = 1
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobserver @job_id = @jobId, @server_name = N'(local)'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
COMMIT TRANSACTION
GOTO EndSave
QuitWithRollback:
    IF (@@TRANCOUNT > 0) ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
EndSave:

GO


Copy table to another database, set unique name and drop old tables


USE [konhri2]
GO

-- Table should not exist
IF NOT EXISTS (select * from sysobjects where name='apslog_temporary_name' and xtype='U')
	CREATE TABLE apslog_temporary_name(
		[logid] [int] IDENTITY(1,1) NOT FOR REPLICATION NOT NULL,
		[Node] [char](4) NOT NULL,
		[Maintenance] [bit] NOT NULL,
		[MaintDesc] [varchar](50) NULL,
		[NodeStatus] [varchar](20) NOT NULL,
		[TimeChanged] [datetime] NOT NULL,
		[UserChanged] [varchar](50) NOT NULL,
		[Program] [varchar](200) NOT NULL,
		[TrgMethod] [varchar](20) NULL
		)
GO

-- Copy data
SET IDENTITY_INSERT apslog_temporary_name ON
GO

INSERT INTO	apslog_temporary_name([logid],[Node],[Maintenance],[MaintDesc],[NodeStatus],[TimeChanged],[UserChanged],[Program],[TrgMethod])
SELECT	[logid],[Node],[Maintenance],[MaintDesc],[NodeStatus],[TimeChanged],[UserChanged],[Program],[TrgMethod]
FROM	konhri.dbo.APSLog
GO

SET IDENTITY_INSERT apslog_temporary_name OFF
GO

-- Change name to a unique one
DECLARE @tablename nvarchar(50)
SET @tablename = 'apslog_' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), GETDATE(), 112) + '_' + REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),108), ':', '')
exec sp_rename 'apslog_temporary_name', @tablename


-- Remove tables older than XX days
DECLARE @sql NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'';

;WITH p(o) AS
(
	select	name 
	from	sys.tables
	where	create_date <  dateadd(mi, -5, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
)
SELECT @sql += 'DROP TABLE ' + o + ';' FROM p

exec sp_executesql @sql



Kill job if it is running

USE [msdb]
GO

/****** Object:  Job [Kill long running MP_FMP_IndexOptimizeReorganize_UserDB]    Script Date: 2016-10-21 10:30:22 ******/
BEGIN TRANSACTION
DECLARE @ReturnCode INT
SELECT @ReturnCode = 0
/****** Object:  JobCategory [[Uncategorized (Local)]]]    Script Date: 2016-10-21 10:30:22 ******/
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT name FROM msdb.dbo.syscategories WHERE name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]' AND category_class=1)
BEGIN
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_category @class=N'JOB', @type=N'LOCAL', @name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback

END

DECLARE @jobId BINARY(16)
EXEC @ReturnCode =  msdb.dbo.sp_add_job @job_name=N'Kill long running MP_FMP_IndexOptimizeReorganize_UserDB', 
		@enabled=1, 
		@notify_level_eventlog=0, 
		@notify_level_email=0, 
		@notify_level_netsend=0, 
		@notify_level_page=0, 
		@delete_level=0, 
		@description=N'MP_FMP_IndexOptimizeReorganize_UserDB job should not run during daytime', 
		@category_name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]', 
		@owner_login_name=N'sa', @job_id = @jobId OUTPUT
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Check if job is running]    Script Date: 2016-10-21 10:30:22 ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Check if job is running', 
		@step_id=1, 
		@cmdexec_success_code=0, 
		@on_success_action=1, 
		@on_success_step_id=0, 
		@on_fail_action=3, 
		@on_fail_step_id=0, 
		@retry_attempts=0, 
		@retry_interval=0, 
		@os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
		@command=N'use msdb 
declare @rm integer

set @rm = (
SELECT
    count(job.name)
    --,job.job_id, 
    --job.originating_server, 
    --activity.run_requested_date, 
    --DATEDIFF( SECOND, activity.run_requested_date, GETDATE() ) as Elapsed
FROM msdb.dbo.sysjobs_view job
JOIN msdb.dbo.sysjobactivity activity ON   job.job_id = activity.job_id
JOIN msdb.dbo.syssessions sess ON sess.session_id = activity.session_id
JOIN
(
    SELECT
        MAX( agent_start_date ) AS max_agent_start_date
    FROM
        msdb.dbo.syssessions
) sess_max
ON sess.agent_start_date = sess_max.max_agent_start_date
WHERE run_requested_date IS NOT NULL 
AND stop_execution_date IS NULL
AND job.name = ''MP_FMP_IndexOptimizeReorganize_UserDB'' )

IF (@rm = 1)
RAISERROR (''Killing job MP_FMP_IndexOptimizeReorganize_UserDB'', 16, 1)
', 
		@database_name=N'msdb', 
		@flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Kill the job running]    Script Date: 2016-10-21 10:30:22 ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Kill the job running', 
		@step_id=2, 
		@cmdexec_success_code=0, 
		@on_success_action=1, 
		@on_success_step_id=0, 
		@on_fail_action=2, 
		@on_fail_step_id=0, 
		@retry_attempts=0, 
		@retry_interval=0, 
		@os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
		@command=N'USE msdb
GO
EXEC dbo.sp_stop_job
N''MP_FMP_IndexOptimizeReorganize_UserDB''', 
		@database_name=N'msdb', 
		@flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_update_job @job_id = @jobId, @start_step_id = 1
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobserver @job_id = @jobId, @server_name = N'(local)'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
COMMIT TRANSACTION
GOTO EndSave
QuitWithRollback:
    IF (@@TRANCOUNT > 0) ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
EndSave:

GO







Backup and restore
Basic information on backupfile, database and database files
SELECT  
   bs.database_name,  
   bmf.physical_device_name,
   dbfiles.physical_name
FROM	msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily bmf,
		msdb.dbo.backupset bs,
		sys.databases sysdb,
		sys.master_files dbfiles  
WHERE   bs.media_set_id = bmf.media_set_id
AND		bs.database_name = sysdb.name
AND		sysdb.database_id = dbfiles.database_id
AND		(CONVERT(datetime, bs.backup_start_date, 102) >= dateadd(hour, -2, getdate()))
AND     bs.type = 'D' --D=full, I=Diff, L=Logg
ORDER BY bs.database_name, bs.backup_finish_date

Backup all databases script

DECLARE @name VARCHAR(50) -- database name  
DECLARE @path VARCHAR(256) -- path for backup files  
DECLARE @fileName VARCHAR(256) -- filename for backup  
DECLARE @fileDate VARCHAR(20) -- used for file name
 
-- specify database backup directory
SET @path = 'Z:\DBBackup\FULL\'  
 
-- specify filename format
SELECT @fileDate = CONVERT(VARCHAR(20),GETDATE(),112) 
--SELECT CONVERT(VARCHAR(20),GETDATE(),112) 
 
DECLARE db_cursor CURSOR READ_ONLY FOR  
SELECT name 
FROM master.dbo.sysdatabases 
WHERE name NOT IN ('tempdb')  -- exclude these databases
 
OPEN db_cursor   
FETCH NEXT FROM db_cursor INTO @name   
 
WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0   
BEGIN   
   SET @fileName = @path + @name + '_' + @fileDate + '.BAK'  
   BACKUP DATABASE @name TO DISK = @fileName  WITH COMPRESSION
 
   FETCH NEXT FROM db_cursor INTO @name   
END   

 
CLOSE db_cursor   
DEALLOCATE db_cursor


Create restore script

SET NOCOUNT ON

DECLARE @MoveOption AS TABLE
           ([Id] INT IDENTITY(1,1),
           [MoveOption] VARCHAR(MAX))

PRINT 'RESTORE DATABASE [' + DB_NAME(DB_ID()) + ']'
--     Enter backup file name
PRINT 'FROM DISK = N''X:\DBBackup\TEMP\xxxx.bak''' 
PRINT 'WITH NORECOVERY, FILE = 1,'
PRINT ''

INSERT INTO @MoveOption ([MoveOption])
SELECT
    'MOVE N''' + a.name + ''' TO N''' + a.FILENAME + ''',' AS [MOVE OPTION]
FROM
    dbo.sysfiles a

DECLARE 
            @LastId INT = 0,
           @MoveOptionText VARCHAR(MAX)

WHILE EXISTS (SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM @MoveOption WHERE [Id] > @LastId)
BEGIN
           SELECT TOP 1
                    @MoveOptionText = [MoveOption], 
                    @LastId = [Id]
           FROM       @MoveOption
           WHERE      [Id] > @LastId
           ORDER BY [Id] ASC

           PRINT @MoveOptionText
END

PRINT 'NOUNLOAD, STATS = 10'
PRINT 'GO'

Create restore scripts from msdb

DECLARE @databaseName sysname
DECLARE @backupStartDate datetime 
DECLARE @backup_set_id_start INT 
DECLARE @backup_set_id_end INT 

-- Loop over DB name
DECLARE C CURSOR FOR
SELECT [name] FROM sys.databases WHERE [name] not in ('msdb','master','model','tempdb')

OPEN C
FETCH NEXT FROM C INTO @databaseName
WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN
	
	SELECT @backup_set_id_start = MAX(backup_set_id) 
	FROM msdb.dbo.backupset 
	WHERE database_name = @databaseName AND type = 'D' 

	SELECT @backup_set_id_end = MIN(backup_set_id) 
	FROM msdb.dbo.backupset 
	WHERE database_name = @databaseName AND type = 'D' 
	AND backup_set_id > @backup_set_id_start 

	IF @backup_set_id_end IS NULL SET @backup_set_id_end = 999999999 

	SELECT backup_set_id, 'RESTORE DATABASE ' + @databaseName + ' FROM DISK = ''' + mf.physical_device_name + ''' WITH NORECOVERY' 
	FROM msdb.dbo.backupset b, 
	msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily mf 
	WHERE b.media_set_id = mf.media_set_id 
	AND b.database_name = @databaseName 
	AND b.backup_set_id = @backup_set_id_start 
	UNION 
	SELECT backup_set_id, 'RESTORE LOG ' + @databaseName + ' FROM DISK = ''' + mf.physical_device_name + ''' WITH NORECOVERY' 
	FROM msdb.dbo.backupset b, 
	msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily mf 
	WHERE b.media_set_id = mf.media_set_id 
	AND b.database_name = @databaseName 
	AND b.backup_set_id >= @backup_set_id_start AND b.backup_set_id < @backup_set_id_end 
	AND b.type = 'L' 
	UNION 
	SELECT 999999999 AS backup_set_id, 'RESTORE DATABASE ' + @databaseName + ' WITH RECOVERY' 
	ORDER BY backup_set_id

	FETCH NEXT FROM C INTO @databaseName
END
CLOSE C
DEALLOCATE C
Create restore scripts from msdb information, both Litespeed and native
--Tips/kom ihåg
Sätt query results resultatlängd till mer än 256 tecken per rad, annars klipper den koden

--Vilka databaser har inte backats sen en viss tid

select * from sys.databases
where   name not in(SELECT bs.database_name
					FROM	msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily bmf,
							msdb.dbo.backupset bs
					WHERE   bs.media_set_id = bmf.media_set_id
					AND		(CONVERT(datetime, bs.backup_start_date, 102) 

>= dateadd(hour, -15, getdate()))
					AND     bs.type = 'D' --D=full, I=Diff
					)




------------------------------------------
--Restora Litespeed fullbackup

SELECT bs.database_name, 'exec master.dbo.xp_restore_database @database = N''' +
bs.database_name + ''', @filename = N''' + bmf.physical_device_name + ''', ' +
'@filenumber = 1, @with = N''REPLACE'', 
@with = N''STATS = 10'', 
@with = N''NORECOVERY'', 
@affinity = 0, @logging = 0 '
+ CHAR(10) + 'GO' + CHAR(10)
FROM	msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily bmf,
		msdb.dbo.backupset bs
WHERE   bs.media_set_id = bmf.media_set_id
AND		(CONVERT(datetime, bs.backup_start_date, 102) >= dateadd(hour, -15, getdate()))
AND     bs.type = 'D' --D=full, I=Diff
and		bs.database_name like '%OPTAC%'
order by bs.database_name


------------------------------------------------

--Restore Diff backup, native backup


SELECT  'RESTORE DATABASE [' + bs.database_name + ']
FROM DISK = N''' + bmf.physical_device_name + '''' +
' WITH  FILE = 1,  
NOUNLOAD,  
REPLACE,  
STATS = 50'
+ CHAR(10) + 'GO' + CHAR(10)
FROM	msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily bmf,
		msdb.dbo.backupset bs
WHERE   bs.media_set_id = bmf.media_set_id
AND	(CONVERT(datetime, bs.backup_start_date, 102) >= dateadd(hour, -8, getdate()))
AND	bs.database_name like '%OPTAC%'
AND     bs.type = 'D' --D=full, I=Diff





Restore all logs in a directory
DECLARE @Path nvarchar(260) 
SET @Path = 'W:\DBBackup\TEMP\LOG' 
 
IF RIGHT(@Path, 1) <> '\' 
   SET @Path = @Path + '\' 
 
IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#dirtree') IS NOT NULL 
  DROP TABLE #dirtree 

CREATE TABLE #dirtree( 
  id int IDENTITY, 
  directory nvarchar(260), 
  depth int, 
  IsFile bit
) 

INSERT #dirtree EXEC master.dbo.xp_dirtree @path = @Path, @depth = 0, @file = 1 

DECLARE @depth int, @depthMax int 
UPDATE #dirtree SET directory = @Path + directory WHERE depth = 1 

DECLARE @RestorePath nvarchar(4000) 
DECLARE C CURSOR FOR SELECT directory FROM #dirtree order by directory asc
OPEN C 
FETCH NEXT FROM C INTO @RestorePath 
WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS=0 
BEGIN 
   PRINT 'Restoring ' + @RestorePath
  RESTORE LOG [TEST] FROM DISK=@RestorePath WITH NORECOVERY 
  FETCH NEXT FROM C INTO @RestorePath 
END 
CLOSE C 
DEALLOCATE C

Multiple restore of Litespeed backups
-- Scriptet gör restore på flera databaser (alla *.bak filer placerade i katalogen angiven i @str_bakfile_path).
-- Hanterar bara en mdf och en ldf per databas.
-- Fungerar på SQL 2005 och 2008, testat med litespeed v5.2.0.1240
use master

exec sp_configure 'show advanced options', '1'
reconfigure
GO
exec sp_configure 'xp_cmdshell', '1'
reconfigure
GO

-- deklarera variabler
declare @str_bakfile_path nvarchar(255)
declare @str_datafile_path nvarchar(255)
declare @str_logfile_path nvarchar(255)

declare @int_currentid int
declare @str_currentbakfile nvarchar(255)
declare @str_currentlogicalname_data nvarchar(255)
declare @str_currentlogicalname_log nvarchar(255)
declare @str_currentdbname nvarchar(255)
declare @str_restoredb_command nvarchar(512)

declare @str_getbakfiles_command nvarchar(255)
declare @str_getfilelistonly_command nvarchar(255)
declare @str_getheaderonly_command nvarchar(255)

declare @tbl_bakfiles table(id int identity, [filename] nvarchar(255), logical_data nvarchar(255), logical_log nvarchar(255), [dbname] nvarchar(255), completed bit)
declare @tbl_filelistonly_result table(LogicalName nvarchar(128), PhysicalName nvarchar(128), [Type] char(1), FileGroupName nvarchar(128), Size nvarchar(128), MaxSize nvarchar(128))
declare @tbl_headeronly_result table(FileNumber Int, BackupFormat nvarchar(128), Guid Uniqueidentifier, BackupName nvarchar(128), BackupDescription nvarchar(128), BackupType nvarchar(128), ExpirationDate Datetime, Compressed Tinyint, Position Smallint, DeviceType	Tinyint, UserName nvarchar(128), ServerName nvarchar(128), DatabaseName nvarchar(128), DatabaseVersion int, DatabaseCreationDate datetime, BackupSize numeric (20,0), FirstLSN numeric (25,0), LastLSN numeric (25,0), CheckpointLSN numeric (25,0), DifferentialBaseLSN numeric (25,0), BackupStartDate datetime, BackupFinishDate datetime, SortOrder Smallint, CodePage Smallint, CompatibilityLevel Tinyint, SoftwareVendorId Int, SoftwareVersionMajor Int, SoftwareVersionMinor Int, SoftwareVersionBuild Int, MachineName nvarchar(128), BindingID Uniqueidentifier, RecoveryForkID Uniqueidentifier, Encryption Int, IsCopyOnly nvarchar(128))

-- sökväg till bakfiler samt sökvägar för data och loggfiler vid restore
set @str_bakfile_path = 'x:\temp\baks\'
set @str_datafile_path = 'x:\temp\datafiles\'
set @str_logfile_path = 'x:\temp\logfiles\'

set @str_getbakfiles_command = 'dir /B ' + @str_bakfile_path + '*.bak'

-- fyll tabell med *.bak filer
insert into @tbl_bakfiles([filename]) exec xp_cmdshell @str_getbakfiles_command
update @tbl_bakfiles set [filename]=@str_bakfile_path + [filename], completed = 0
delete @tbl_bakfiles where [filename] is NULL

-- fyll på tabell med logiska filnamn samt dbnamn
while exists (select * from @tbl_bakfiles where completed = 0)
begin
    select top 1 @int_currentid = id, @str_currentbakfile = [filename] from @tbl_bakfiles where completed = 0 order by id asc

	set @str_getfilelistonly_command = 'exec master.dbo.xp_restore_filelistonly @filename = ''' + @str_currentbakfile + ''''
	set @str_getheaderonly_command = 'exec master.dbo.xp_restore_headeronly @filename = ''' + @str_currentbakfile + ''''
	
	insert @tbl_filelistonly_result exec (@str_getfilelistonly_command)
	insert @tbl_headeronly_result exec (@str_getheaderonly_command)
	
   	update @tbl_bakfiles set logical_data = (select logicalname from @tbl_filelistonly_result where Type = 'D') where id = @int_currentid
   	update @tbl_bakfiles set logical_log = (select logicalname from @tbl_filelistonly_result where Type = 'L') where id = @int_currentid
   	update @tbl_bakfiles set dbname = (select DatabaseName from @tbl_headeronly_result) where id = @int_currentid

	update @tbl_bakfiles set completed = 1 where id = @int_currentid
	delete @tbl_filelistonly_result
	delete @tbl_headeronly_result
end

update @tbl_bakfiles set completed = 0
select * from @tbl_bakfiles

-- gör restore
while exists (select * from @tbl_bakfiles where completed = 0)
begin
    select top 1 @int_currentid = id, @str_currentbakfile = [filename], @str_currentlogicalname_data = logical_data, @str_currentlogicalname_log = logical_log, @str_currentdbname = dbname from @tbl_bakfiles where completed = 0 order by id asc

	select @str_restoredb_command = 'EXEC master.dbo.xp_restore_database @database = N''' + @str_currentdbname + ''', @filename = ''' + @str_currentbakfile + ''', @with = N''REPLACE'', @with = N''MOVE N'''''+ @str_currentlogicalname_data +''''' TO N''''' + @str_datafile_path + @str_currentdbname + '.mdf'''''', @with = N''MOVE N''''' + @str_currentlogicalname_log +''''' TO N''''' + @str_logfile_path + @str_currentdbname + '_log.ldf'''''', @logging = 0'
	exec (@str_restoredb_command)

	update @tbl_bakfiles set completed = 1 where id = @int_currentid
end

exec sp_configure 'xp_cmdshell', '0'
reconfigure
GO
exec sp_configure 'show advanced options', '0'
reconfigure
GO

-- Scriptet gör restore på flera databaser (alla *.bak filer placerade i katalogen angiven i @str_bakfile_path).
-- Hanterar bara en mdf och en ldf per databas.
-- Fungerar på SQL 2005 och 2008.
use master

exec sp_configure 'show advanced options', '1'

exec sp_configure 'xp_cmdshell', '1'
