Determine the members of database roles
 SELECT 'Database Role' AS Type, Role.name AS RoleName, 
Member.name AS MemberName 
FROM sys.database_role_members AS DRM
JOIN sys.database_principals AS Member
    ON DRM.member_principal_id = Member.principal_ID
RIGHT OUTER JOIN sys.database_principals AS Role
    ON DRM.role_principal_id = Role.principal_id
--WHERE Role.type_desc = 'DATABASE_ROLE'
ORDER BY RoleName, MemberName;
 
Determine the members of server roles
 SELECT 'Server Role' AS Type, Role.name AS RoleName, 
Member.name AS MemberName 
FROM sys.server_role_members AS SRM
JOIN sys.server_principals AS Member
    ON SRM.member_principal_id = Member.principal_ID
RIGHT OUTER JOIN sys.server_principals AS Role
    ON SRM.role_principal_id = Role.principal_id
--WHERE Role.type_desc = 'SERVER_ROLE'
ORDER BY Type, Role.name;

Display the members of each resource class
SELECT l.name AS [member], r.name AS [server role]
FROM sys.server_role_members AS rm
JOIN sys.server_principals AS l
    ON l.principal_id = rm.member_principal_id
JOIN sys.server_principals AS r
    ON r.principal_id = rm.role_principal_id
WHERE l.[type] = 'S' 
    AND r.[type] = 'R'
    AND r.name IN ('mediumrc', 'largerc', 'xlargerc' );

--------
SELECT l.name AS [member], r.name AS [server role]
FROM sys.server_role_members AS rm
JOIN sys.server_principals AS l
  ON l.principal_id = rm.member_principal_id
JOIN
  sys.server_principals AS r
  ON r.principal_id = rm.role_principal_id
WHERE
  ( l.[type] = 'S' OR l.[type] = 'U' OR l.[type] = 'G' )
  AND r.[type] = 'R'
  AND r.[name] in ('mediumrc', 'largerc', 'xlargerc');
Find the number of active requests for each resource group
SELECT g.group_id, g.name AS GroupName, 
COALESCE ( s.SessionCount, 0 ) AS ConnectedSessions,
g.active_request_count AS ActiveRequests
FROM sys.dm_pdw_nodes_resource_governor_workload_groups AS g
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(
    SELECT group_id, SessionCount = COUNT(*)
    FROM sys.dm_pdw_nodes_exec_sessions
    GROUP BY group_id
) AS s
ON g.group_id = s.group_id;

Determine if my requests need more memory
SELECT pr.request_id, [mem].granted_memory_kb, 
[mem].requested_memory_kb, [mem].ideal_memory_kb, 
( [mem].ideal_memory_kb - [mem].granted_memory_kb )/( 1024 * 1024 ) 
    AS requested_memory_gap
-- Get all pdw requests
FROM sys.dm_pdw_exec_requests AS pr
-- For each request_id, add the associated SQL requests on the Compute nodes.
JOIN sys.dm_pdw_sql_requests AS psqlr 
    ON psqlr.request_id = pr.request_id
-- Reduce the results to only return requests that are 
-- currently running in my session.
JOIN sys.dm_pdw_nodes_exec_requests AS sqlr 
    ON sqlr.[session_id] = psqlr.[spid]
        AND sqlr.[pdw_node_id] = psqlr.[pdw_node_id]
-- Add in the memory grant information
JOIN sys.dm_pdw_nodes_exec_query_memory_grants AS [mem] 
    ON [mem].[session_id] = psqlr.[spid] 
        AND [mem].[pdw_node_id] = psqlr.[pdw_node_id];

Determine Average CPU time per request in each resource pool to date
SELECT p.pool_id, p.name, 
COALESCE ( SUM (t.total_request_count), 0 ) AS total_request_count,
COALESCE (SUM ( t.total_cpu_usage_ms), 0 ) AS total_cpu_usage_ms,
CASE WHEN SUM (t.total_request_count ) > 0 
     THEN SUM ( t.total_cpu_usage_ms) / SUM (t.total_request_count ) 
     ELSE 0 
END
FROM sys.dm_pdw_nodes_resource_governor_resource_pools AS p
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(
    SELECT g.pool_id, g.total_request_count, g.total_cpu_usage_ms
    FROM sys.dm_pdw_nodes_resource_governor_workload_groups AS g
    WHERE g.pool_id > 1
) AS t
ON p.pool_id = t.pool_id
GROUP BY p.pool_id, p.name;
 
Definition of stored procedure
 SELECT pr.name, modu.definition 
FROM sys.procedures AS pr
JOIN sys.sql_modules AS modu
    ON pr.object_ID = modu.object_id
WHERE pr.name = 'sys.sp_addrolemember';
 
Search in stored procedures
SELECT DISTINCT 
      A.NAME AS OBJECT_NAME,
      A.TYPE_DESC
      FROM SYS.SQL_MODULES M 
      INNER JOIN SYS.OBJECTS A ON M.OBJECT_ID = A.OBJECT_ID
      WHERE M.DEFINITION LIKE '%GetDataTypeValue%'
      ORDER BY TYPE_DESC

Find the request_id for the backup or restore 

Find the request_id for the backup or restore in the sys.dm_pdw_exec_requests (SQL Server PDW) view by finding the backup or restore command in the command column for this view.

For example,
*/
SELECT request_id, status, command FROM sys.dm_pdw_exec_requests;
 
/*
The preceding command returns the command text and the current status, plus request_id. 

Use the request_id to retrieve additional information for the backup or restore, using the sys.pdw_loader_run_stages (SQL Server PDW), sys.pdw_loader_backup_runs (SQL Server PDW), and sys.pdw_loader_backup_run_details (SQL Server PDW) views. For example, this query returns the run_id and information on the start, end, and duration times of the backup or restore, plus any errors, and information on the number of rows processed:
*/
SELECT lbr.run_id, 
er.submit_time, er.end_time, er.total_elapsed_time, er.error_id, lbr.rows_processed 
FROM sys.dm_pdw_exec_requests er 
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
sys.pdw_loader_backup_runs lbr 
ON (er.request_id=lbr.requst_id) 
WHERE er.request_id=’12738’;

Medvetet segt APS script i loop
declare @i int;
declare @stop int;
set @i = 0;
set @stop = 1000000000;

WHILE (@i < @stop) 
BEGIN 

    set @i = @i + 1;
	 select count(*)
    from sys.databases d
    where not exists (select *
				from sys.pdw_loader_backup_runs plbr
				where d.name = plbr.database_name
				and operation_type = 'BACKUP' 
				and status = 'COMPLETED' 
				and mode = 'FULL' 
				and end_time > dateadd(mi, -300, getdate())
				)
    and d.name NOT IN ('master','tempdb','DB_d1fc4d4cfd6a463eb0d46c3d64ff1ef6') 
    AND d.name NOT LIKE '%LOCAL%' 
    AND d.name NOT LIKE '%Precommit%' 
    AND d.name NOT LIKE '%GUID%' -- exclude these databases
	-- Vänta 5 sekunder
    PRINT 'Loop number: ' + CAST(@i as varchar);

END

Powershell och andra Windows script

SQL Server Express scripts
DBCC via Task Scheduler

# Run checkdb on all databases
$ServerInstance = "localhost"
$DateTimestring = Get-Date -Format yyyyMMdd
$logFilePath = "W:\SQLBackup\scripts\CHECKDBOutput\"

$sendOnlyFailed = 1 # Set to 0 to send status for all checks

# Send status email
Function StatusEmail {
    Param([string]$mailstring)
    Send-MailMessage -SmtpServer "YOU SMTP SERVER" -From "SERVERNAME OR SIMILAR" -To @('LIST OF EMAIL ADRESSES') -Subject "DBCC CHECKDB" -Body $mailstring
}

$sqldatabases = "select name from sys.databases"
$dbs = (Invoke-Sqlcmd -Serverinstance $ServerInstance -Query $sqldatabases).name

foreach ($db in $dbs){
    $logFile = $logFilePath+"CHECKDB_Log_"+$db+"_"+$DateTimestring+".txt"
    $sqlcheckdb = "dbcc checkdb($db) with all_errormsgs"
    Invoke-Sqlcmd -Serverinstance $ServerInstance -Query $sqlcheckdb -QueryTimeout 0 -Verbose *> $logFile

    #Read logfile and if we do not have "CHECKDB found 0 allocation errors and 0 consistency errors in database" somewhere we have an error
    $searchForError = "CHECKDB found 0 allocation errors and 0 consistency errors in database"
    if(Select-String -Pattern $searchForError -Path $logFile) {
        $parameters = @{
        'LogName'  = 'Application'
        'EventId'  = 8957
        'EntryType'  = 'Information'
        'Source' = 'MSSQL$SQLEXPRESS'
        'Message'  = 'DBCC CheckDB success'
        }
        Write-EventLog @parameters
        if($sendOnlyFailed -eq 0) {
            StatusEmail "DBCC CHECKDB for database $db succeeded"
        }
    }else{
        $parameters = @{
        'LogName'  = 'Application'
        'EventId'  = 8957
        'EntryType'  = 'Error'
        'Source' = 'MSSQL$SQLEXPRESS'
        'Message'  = 'DBCC CheckDB failed, please check SQL error log'
        }
        Write-EventLog @parameters
        StatusEmail "DBCC CHECKDB for database $db failed, investigate!"
    }
}

# Delete old log files, older than 15 days
$limit = (Get-Date).AddDays(-15)
Get-ChildItem -Path $logFilePath -Recurse -Force | Where-Object { !$_.PSIsContainer -and $_.CreationTime -lt $limit } | Remove-Item

Kopiera backuper mellan SQL Servrar
# Must use a Global login that is pre-created at each server
# Run this script as a Scheduled Task

# Från XDS 
# SESOFMS9071 X:\DBBackup\FULL\SESOFMS9071 (Alla databaser förutom master, model ,msdb) 

# Till Global
# SESOFMS0092 X:\Backup_from_XDS

# På SESOFMS0092 X:\Backup_from_XDS\old ska tommas vid varje replikering 
# och backup som finns under X:\Backup_from_XDS ska flyttas till mappen old innan replikering sker


# First delete oldest backups
$OLDPath = "\\SESOFMS0092\x$\Backup_from_XDS\old\"
$OLDPathSESOFMS9071 = "\\SESOFMS0092\x$\Backup_from_XDS\old\SESOFMS9071\"
Get-ChildItem -Path $OLDPath -Recurse -Include *.bak -File | foreach { $_.Delete() }

# Next move last backups to old catalog
Move-Item \\SESOFMS0092\x$\Backup_from_XDS\SESOFMS9071\*.bak $OLDPathSESOFMS9071

########## Common variables for all copying ##########

$user = "RemoteFilecopy" 
$password = "XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX"
$db = "msdb"
$DestinationCatalogSESOFMS9071 = "\\SESOFMS0092\x$\Backup_from_XDS\SESOFMS9071\"


########## COPY LATEST BACKUPS FROM SESOFMS9071 ##########

$ServerInstance1 = "sesofms9071.xds.scania.com"
$SourceBaseCatalog = "\\sesofms9071.xds.scania.com\x$\"

# Check latest backup from each server and get the latest one
$sqlstring = @"
SELECT	    bmf.physical_device_name [PATHNAME], bs.database_name [DBNAME], right(physical_device_name, charindex('\',reverse(physical_device_name))-1) [FILENAME]
FROM		msdb.dbo.backupmediafamily bmf
INNER JOIN	msdb.dbo.backupset bs ON bmf.media_set_id = bs.media_set_id
WHERE		bs.type = 'D' 
AND		    bs.database_name NOT IN ('master', 'model' ,'msdb', 'tempdb')
AND			cast(bs.backup_finish_date as date) = dateadd(dd,-1, datediff(dd,0,getdate()))
ORDER BY	bs.database_name ASC
"@

$ds = Invoke-Sqlcmd -Username $user -Password $password -Serverinstance $ServerInstance1 -Database $db -Query $sqlstring

foreach ($fname in $ds) 
{
    $FullFilename = $SourceBaseCatalog + $fname.PATHNAME.Remove(0,3)
    $FILENAME = $fname.FILENAME
    #Write-Host $FullFilename

    Set-Location C:

    $DestinationFilename = "$DestinationCatalogSESOFMS9071" + $FILENAME
    #Write-host $DestinationFilename

    # Check if file exists, exit otherwise
    IF(!(Test-Path -path $FullFilename))
	    {Write-Host "$FullFilename not found!"; exit}

    # Check if destination catalogexists, exit otherwise
    IF(!(Test-Path -path $DestinationCatalogSESOFMS9071)) 
        {Write-Host "$DestinationCatalogSESOFMS9071 not found, exiting`r`n"; exit}

    # Copy file from XDS server to Global server
    #Write-Host "Start copying file $FullFilename to $DestinationFilename"
    Copy-Item -Path $FullFilename -Destination $DestinationFilename 

}

Drop AO databases and restore new with AO setup
$Instance1 = "PWT-A-SQL01" # Where we will do the restore
$Instance2 = "PWT-A-SQL02"
$db = "dbUmoYoumoEpiStage"
$AGName = "PWT-A-SQLAG01"
$Primary = ""
$Secondary = ""

$DatabaseBackupFile = "\\$Instance1\hadrbackup\$db.bak"
$LogBackupFile = "\\$Instance1\hadrbackup\$db.trn"
$AGPrimaryPath = "SQLSERVER:\SQL\$Instance1\DEFAULT\AvailabilityGroups\$AGName"
$AGSecondaryPath = "SQLSERVER:\SQL\$Instance2\DEFAULT\AvailabilityGroups\$AGName"

$umohost="pwtstage.umo.se"
$umohostserver1="pwtstage1.umo.se"
$umohostserver2="pwtstage2.umo.se"
$youmohost="pwtstage.youmo.se"
$users = "win\svcpwtepia", "win\PWTSQLAdmins" 
$restorefile = (gci D:\Nogui\psftp\Downloads).FullName | select -last 1

#
# Drop database ($db) involves removing DB from AG, dropping on both nodes and restoring on both nodes
# Temporarily disabling Log backup job as it can interfere in the setup of db on secondary node
#

# Check if cluster is ok before moving on
$sqlClusterCheck = "SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt
FROM sys.dm_hadr_database_replica_states AS drs
INNER JOIN sys.availability_databases_cluster AS adc 
	ON drs.group_id = adc.group_id AND drs.group_database_id = adc.group_database_id
WHERE drs.synchronization_state_desc = 'SYNCHRONIZED'
AND drs.synchronization_health_desc = 'HEALTHY'
AND adc.database_name = '${db}'
"
$clusterCheckOK = (Invoke-Sqlcmd -ServerInstance $AGName -Query $sqlClusterCheck).cnt
if($clusterCheckOK -eq 2) {
    Write-Host "Cluster looks ok, moving on"
}else{
    Write-Host "Cluster NOT ok, investigate, exiting"
    exit
} 


# Check which server is primary, synchronization_health only have a value on the primary server
$sqlCheckPrimary = @"
SELECT synchronization_health [Synch]
FROM sys.dm_hadr_availability_replica_states ars
WHERE is_local = 1 and role_desc = 'PRIMARY'
"@


$Synch = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Instance1 -Query $sqlCheckPrimary).Synch
if($Synch.length -eq 0 ) {
    $Synch = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Instance2 -Query $sqlCheckPrimary).Synch
    if($Synch.length -eq 0 ) {
        # No primary found, we exit
        write-host "No primary found, exiting!"
        exit
    }else{
        $Primary = $Instance2
        $Secondary = $Instance1
    }
}else{
        $Primary = $Instance1
        $Secondary = $Instance2
}

write-host "$Primary is primary and $Secondary is secondary"

# Remove DB from AG
$sqlRemoveDBFromAG = "ALTER AVAILABILITY GROUP [${AGName}] REMOVE DATABASE [${db}]"
write-host "Remove database from AG"
Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Primary -Query $sqlRemoveDBFromAG

# Drop database from Primary and Secondary
$sqlDropDBOnPrimary = "Use master;
ALTER DATABASE [${db}] SET SINGLE_USER WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE;
DROP DATABASE [${db}]"
write-host "Drop DB from primary"
Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Primary -Query $sqlDropDBOnPrimary -QueryTimeout 0


Try{
    $sqlDropDBOnSecondary = "Use master;
    ALTER DATABASE [${db}] SET SINGLE_USER WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE;
    go
    DROP DATABASE [${db}];
    go"
    write-host "Drop DB from secondary"
    Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Secondary -Query $sqlDropDBOnSecondary -QueryTimeout 0 -ErrorAction Stop
}Catch{
    write-host "Nope did not work on first try, sleep 30 sec to let AO sync status, then try drop again"
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 30

    $sqlCheckExists = "select count(name) as cnt from sys.databases where name = '${db}'"
    $cnt = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Secondary -Query $sqlCheckExists).cnt

    if($cnt -gt 0) {

        #Check if it is in restoring mode
        $sqlState = "select state from sys.databases where name = '${db}'"
        $state = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Secondary -Query $sqlState).state

        if($state -eq 1) {
            $sqlDropDBOnSecondary = "Use master;
            RESTORE DATABASE [${db}] WITH RECOVERY;
            go
            DROP DATABASE [${db}];
            go"

            write-host "Again try to drop DB from secondary"
            Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Secondary -Query $sqlDropDBOnSecondary -ErrorAction Stop
        }else{
            write-host "Secondary database in unknown state, can not drop, exiting!"
            exit
        }
    }
}



# If $Primary is not $Instance1 then failover to it
if($Primary -ne $Instance1) {
    $sqlFailoverAG = "ALTER AVAILABILITY GROUP [${AGName}] FAILOVER;"
    Write-Host "Failover to $Instance1"
    Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $Instance1 -Query $sqlFailoverAG -QueryTimeout 0
}


# Temporarily disable LOG backup jobs
$sqlDisableJob = "msdb.dbo.sp_update_job @job_name='DatabaseBackup - USER_DATABASES - LOG',@enabled = 0"
invoke-sqlcmd -query $sqlDisableJob -serverinstance $Instance1
invoke-sqlcmd -query $sqlDisableJob -serverinstance $Instance2
write-host "Log backup jobs are disabled"

# Restore database on primary
$sqlRestore = "restore database [${db}]
from disk='${restorefile}' with
move 'dbUmoYoumoDev' TO 'D:\Data\MSSQL13.MSSQLSERVER\MSSQL\DATA\${db}.mdf', 
move 'dbUmoYoumoDev_log' TO 'D:\Data\MSSQL13.MSSQLSERVER\MSSQL\DATA\${db}_0.ldf';
GO"
Write-Host "Restoring $restorefile into database $db"
invoke-sqlcmd -query $sqlRestore -serverinstance $Instance1 -QueryTimeout 0


# Delete old HADR backups if they are there
if(Test-Path -Path $DatabaseBackupFile) {
    Remove-Item $DatabaseBackupFile 
}
if(Test-Path -Path $LogBackupFile) {
    Remove-Item $LogBackupFile
}


# Add primary to AG
Write-Host "Add db to primary AG"
Add-SqlAvailabilityDatabase -Path $AGPrimaryPath -Database $db

# Setup secondary database in AG
Write-Host "Create AG backups"
Backup-SqlDatabase -Database $db -BackupFile $DatabaseBackupFile -ServerInstance $Instance1
Backup-SqlDatabase -Database $db -BackupFile $LogBackupFile -ServerInstance $Instance1 -BackupAction Log

Write-Host "Restore AG backups"
Restore-SqlDatabase -Database $db -BackupFile $DatabaseBackupFile -ServerInstance $Instance2 -NoRecovery
Restore-SqlDatabase -Database $db -BackupFile $LogBackupFile -ServerInstance $Instance2 -RestoreAction Log -NoRecovery

Write-Host "Add db to secondary AG after waiting 30 secs"
Start-Sleep -Seconds 30
Add-SqlAvailabilityDatabase -Path $AGSecondaryPath -Database $db


# Enable LOG backup jobs
$sqlEnableJob = "msdb.dbo.sp_update_job @job_name='DatabaseBackup - USER_DATABASES - LOG',@enabled = 1"
invoke-sqlcmd -query $sqlEnableJob -serverinstance $Instance1
invoke-sqlcmd -query $sqlEnableJob -serverinstance $Instance2
write-host "Log backup jobs are enabled"

# Delete HADR backups
Write-Host "Delete HADR backups"
if(Test-Path -Path $DatabaseBackupFile) {
    Remove-Item $DatabaseBackupFile
}
if(Test-Path -Path $LogBackupFile) {
    Remove-Item $LogBackupFile
}




#
# Add users as db_owner
#


Foreach ($user in $users)
{
Write-Host "Adding $user as db_owner in database $db"
    $query = "use ${db}
CREATE USER [${user}] FOR LOGIN [${user}] ;
exec sp_addrolemember 'db_owner', '${user}';
GO"
    invoke-sqlcmd -query $query -serverinstance $instance1
}

#
# Updating Hostdefintions in database
#

$query ="use ${db}
UPDATE [dbo].[tblHostDefinition] SET Name = '${umohost}', Type = 1, Language = 'sv', Https = 1 WHERE Name = 'www.umo.se'
UPDATE [dbo].[tblHostDefinition] SET Name = '${youmohost}', Type = 1, Language = 'sv', Https = 1 WHERE Name = 'www.youmo.se'
UPDATE [dbo].[tblHostDefinition] SET Name = '${umohostserver1}', Type = 0, Language = 'sv', Https = 1 WHERE Name = 'www1.umo.se'
UPDATE [dbo].[tblHostDefinition] SET Name = '${umohostserver2}', Type = 0, Language = 'sv', Https = 1 WHERE Name = 'www2.umo.se'

DELETE FROM [dbo].[tblHostDefinition] WHERE Name = 'umo.se'
DELETE FROM [dbo].[tblHostDefinition] WHERE Name = 'youmo.se'

UPDATE [dbo].[tblSiteDefinition] SET SiteUrl = 'https://${umohost}/' WHERE Name = 'Umo'
UPDATE [dbo].[tblSiteDefinition] SET SiteUrl = 'https://${youmohost}/' WHERE Name = 'Youmo'
GO"

Write-Host "Updating Hostdefintions in database $db"
invoke-sqlcmd -query $query -serverinstance $instance1

Backup, copy and restore FMP database to another server, set up table to allow agent job running dbcc check

Powershell part creating a new backup in primary AO and copying it over to checkdb server

########## Take a backup from FMSFMPDB Primary server to 9340(local) and run checkdb ##########


# Log to file all actions
Function LogWrite {
   Param ([string]$logstring)
   $stamp = Get-Date -Format yyyyMMdd_HHmmss
   $line = "$stamp $logstring"
   Add-content $Logfile -value $line

}

# Send status email
Function StatusEmail {
    Param([string]$mailstring)
    Send-MailMessage -SmtpServer "mailhost.scania.com" -From "sesofms9340@xds.scania.com" -To @('richard@qreed.se','richard.hillstrom@scania.com') -Subject "Status email" -Body $mailstring -Attachments $LogFile
}

$DateTimestring = Get-Date -Format yyyyMMdd_HHmmss
$LogFile = "Z:\DBBackup\Copy_from_prod\Logfile_$DateTimestring.txt"




################### FLEETMANAGEMENT START ###############

LogWrite "-----------------   FLEETMANAGEMENT  ----------------"

# Some global variables for FleetManagement

$SourceInstance1 = "sesofms9292"
$SourceInstance2 = "sesofms9293"
$SourceDatabase = "FleetManagement"
$SourceServer = ""

$TargetInstance  = "sesofms9340"
$TargetDatabase = "FleetManagement_CHECKDB"


# DB connections run mostly under the MP_checkdb_user which can only run backup on prod but have sysadmin on preprod
$BackupLogin = "MP_checkdb_user"
$BackupLoginPassword = "djgKGJD74KJDHGdkruvmeoi4kfj"



# Check which server is primary, synchronization_health only have a value on the primary server
$sqlstring = @"
SELECT synchronization_health [Synch]
FROM sys.dm_hadr_availability_replica_states ars
WHERE is_local = 1 and role_desc = 'PRIMARY'
"@



$Synch = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $SourceInstance1 -Query $sqlstring).Synch
if($Synch.length -eq 0 ) {
    $Synch = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $SourceInstance2 -Query $sqlstring).Synch
    if($Synch.length -eq 0 ) {
        # No primary found, we exit
        LogWrite "No Fleetmanagement primary found, exiting!"
        exit
    }else{
        $SourceServer = $SourceInstance2
    }
}else{
        $SourceServer = $SourceInstance1
}


###############  Now to take a fullbackup with copy only  ###########
$BackupFilename = 'FMSCLUPROD008$FMSAGPROD008_FleetManagement_FULL_COPY_ONLY_'+$DateTimestring+".bak"

# Backup and copy variables
$RemoteDisk = '\z$'
$BackupDisk = "z:"
$BackupFolder = '\DBBackup\FULL\FMSCLUPROD008$FMSAGPROD008_COPY_ONLY\'+$SourceDatabase+'\'
$BackupPathAndName = $BackupDisk+$BackupFolder+$BackupFilename

# Copy from below
$CopyFromPathAndName = '\\'+$SourceServer+$RemoteDisk+$BackupFolder + $BackupFilename

# Copy to below
$Destination = "Z:\DBBackup\Copy_from_prod\"+$BackupFilename

LogWrite "Backup path and file name $CopyFromPathAndName and Local server destination $Destination"

$sqlstring = @"
BACKUP DATABASE [FleetManagement] 
TO  DISK = N'Z:\DBBackup\FULL\FMSCLUPROD008`$FMSAGPROD008_COPY_ONLY\FleetManagement\${BackupFilename}' WITH  COPY_ONLY, NOFORMAT, NOINIT,  NAME = N'FleetManagement-Full Database Backup', 
SKIP, NOREWIND, NOUNLOAD, COMPRESSION,  STATS = 10
"@

LogWrite "Start full backup"
StatusEmail "Start full backup of FMP"

Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $SourceServer -Query $sqlstring -Username $BackupLogin -Password $BackupLoginPassword -QueryTimeout 0

LogWrite "Backup finished"

################### START COPYING DATABASE  ################################

Set-Location C:

LogWrite "Start copying $CopyFromPathAndName to $Destination"
StatusEmail "Copy FMP backup"

# Check if file exists, exit otherwise
IF(!(Test-Path -path $CopyFromPathAndName))
	{LogWrite "Fleetmanagement file to copy not found!"; exit}


# Copy file
try{
    Copy-Item -Path $CopyFromPathAndName -Destination $Destination
}catch{
    LogWrite "Could not copy Fleetmanagement backup, exiting"
    exit
}

LogWrite "Copy file finished"

########## COPY DATABASE DONE ##########





########## START RESTORE DATABASE ##########

$restorestring = @"
USE [master]
if exists(select name from master.sys.databases where name = N'${TargetDatabase}')
alter database ${TargetDatabase} set single_user with rollback immediate

RESTORE DATABASE ${TargetDatabase} 
FROM  DISK = N'${Destination}' WITH  FILE = 1,  
MOVE N'FleetManagement1' TO N'W:\SQLDba\Data\FleetManagement\FleetManagement1',  MOVE N'FleetManagement5' TO N'W:\SQLDba\Data\FleetManagement\FleetManagement5',

MOVE N'FleetManagement_Log' TO N'L:\SQLDba\Log\FleetManagement\FleetManagement_Log',  
MOVE N'FleetManagement_Log2' TO N'L:\SQLDba\Log\FleetManagement\FleetManagement_Log2.ldf',  
NOUNLOAD,  REPLACE,  STATS = 5

alter database ${TargetDatabase} set recovery simple
"@

#Write-Host @sqlstring
LogWrite "Start restore"
StatusEmail "Start restore of FMP"

Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $TargetInstance -Query $restorestring -Username $BackupLogin -Password $BackupLoginPassword -QueryTimeout 0

LogWrite "Restore finished"

LogWrite "Delete backup file $Destination"
Remove-Item $Destination

########## START CREATE STATUS TABLE ##########
$sqlstring = @"
use ${TargetDatabase}
create table CHECK_STATUS(checkrunstatus bit not null)
insert into CHECK_STATUS values(1)
"@

#Write-Host @sqlstring
LogWrite "Create status table"

Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $TargetInstance -Query $sqlstring -Username $BackupLogin -Password $BackupLoginPassword 

LogWrite "Status table created"
LogWrite "FleetManagement done, next is backing up and copying Positioning"
StatusEmail "FMP done"
################### FLEETMANAGEMENT DONE ###############

LogWrite "-----------------   FLEETMANAGEMENTPOSITIONING  ----------------"

$SourceInstance1 = "sesofms9292"
$SourceInstance2 = "sesofms9293"
$SourceDatabase = "FleetManagementPositioning"
$SourceServer = ""

$TargetInstance  = "sesofms9340"
$TargetDatabase = "FleetManagementPositioning_CHECKDB"


# DB connections run mostly under the MP_checkdb_user which can only run backup on prod but have sysadmin on preprod
$BackupLogin = "MP_checkdb_user"
$BackupLoginPassword = "djgKGJD74KJDHGdkruvmeoi4kfj"



# Check which server is primary, synchronization_health only have a value on the primary server
$sqlstring = @"
SELECT synchronization_health [Synch]
FROM sys.dm_hadr_availability_replica_states ars
WHERE is_local = 1 and role_desc = 'PRIMARY'
"@


$Synch = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $SourceInstance1 -Query $sqlstring).Synch
if($Synch.length -eq 0 ) {
    $Synch = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $SourceInstance2 -Query $sqlstring).Synch
    if($Synch.length -eq 0 ) {
        # No primary found, we exit
        LogWrite "No primary FleetManagementPositioning found, exiting!"
        exit
    }else{
        $SourceServer = $SourceInstance2
    }
}else{
        $SourceServer = $SourceInstance1
}


###############  Now to take a fullbackup with copy only  ###########
$BackupFilename = 'FMSCLUPROD008$FMSAGPROD008_FleetManagementPositioning_FULL_COPY_ONLY_'+$DateTimestring+".bak"

# Backup and copy variables
$RemoteDisk = '\z$'
$BackupDisk = "z:"
$BackupFolder = '\DBBackup\FULL\FMSCLUPROD008$FMSAGPROD008_COPY_ONLY\'+$SourceDatabase+'\'
$BackupPathAndName = $BackupDisk+$BackupFolder+$BackupFilename

# Copy from below
$CopyFromPathAndName = '\\'+$SourceServer+$RemoteDisk+$BackupFolder + $BackupFilename

# Copy to below
$Destination = "Z:\DBBackup\Copy_from_prod\"+$BackupFilename

LogWrite "Backup path and file name $CopyFromPathAndName and Local server destination $Destination"

$sqlstring = @"
BACKUP DATABASE [FleetManagementPositioning] 
TO  DISK = N'Z:\DBBackup\FULL\FMSCLUPROD008`$FMSAGPROD008_COPY_ONLY\FleetManagementPositioning\${BackupFilename}' WITH  COPY_ONLY, NOFORMAT, NOINIT,  NAME = N'FleetManagementPositioning-Full Database Backup', 
SKIP, NOREWIND, NOUNLOAD, COMPRESSION,  STATS = 10
"@

LogWrite "Start full backup"
StatusEmail "Start full backup of POS"

Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $SourceServer -Query $sqlstring -Username $BackupLogin -Password $BackupLoginPassword -QueryTimeout 0

LogWrite "Backup finished"

################### START COPYING DATABASE  ################################

Set-Location C:

LogWrite "Start copying $CopyFromPathAndName to $Destination"
StatusEmail "Start copy of POS"

# Check if file exists, exit otherwise
IF(!(Test-Path -path $CopyFromPathAndName))
	{LogWrite "FleetmanagementPositioning file to copy not found!"; exit}


# Copy file
try{
    Copy-Item -Path $CopyFromPathAndName -Destination $Destination
}catch{
    LogWrite "Could not copy FleetmanagementPosition backup, exiting"
    exit
}

LogWrite "Copy file finished"

########## COPY DATABASE DONE ##########


########## WAIT FOR DBCC DONE BEFORE NEXT RESTORE ##########

$dbccsql = @"
if exists (select stmt_executing 
from(
	SELECT 	SUBSTRING(qt.text,(r.statement_start_offset/2) +1,
			(CASE WHEN r.statement_end_offset = -1
					THEN LEN(CONVERT(nvarchar(max), qt.text)) * 2
			ELSE 
					r.statement_end_offset
			END -r.statement_start_offset)/2) AS stmt_executing
	FROM sys.dm_exec_requests r
	CROSS APPLY sys.dm_exec_sql_text(sql_handle) as qt, 
				sys.dm_exec_sessions s
	WHERE r.session_id > 50 
	and r.session_id=s.session_id
	and r.session_id <> @@SPID) as stmtexecuting
WHERE stmt_executing like '%DBCC%')
begin 
	select 1 as result
end
else 
	select 0 as result
"@

$loop = 1
$writesleepmessage = 1

while($loop -eq 1) {
    $res = (Invoke-Sqlcmd  -Serverinstance $TargetInstance -Query $dbccsql -Username $BackupLogin -Password $BackupLoginPassword -QueryTimeout 0).result
    
    if($res -eq 1) {
    
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 60
        if($writesleepmessage -eq 1) {
            LogWrite "Waiting for DBCC to finish...."
            StatusEmail "Waiting for DBCC to finish...."
            $writesleepmessage = 0
        }
    }
    
    if($res -eq 0) {
        $loop = 0
    }
}


########## START RESTORE DATABASE ##########

if exists(select name from master.sys.databases where name = N'${TargetDatabase}')
alter database ${TargetDatabase} set single_user with rollback immediate

MOVE N'FleetManagementPositioning' TO N'W:\SQLDba\Data\FleetManagementPositioning.mdf',

Agent job 

USE [msdb]
GO

/****** Object:  Job [Run CheckDB on database copy]    Script Date: 10/24/2017 2:22:03 PM ******/
BEGIN TRANSACTION
DECLARE @ReturnCode INT
SELECT @ReturnCode = 0
/****** Object:  JobCategory [[Uncategorized (Local)]]    Script Date: 10/24/2017 2:22:03 PM ******/
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT name FROM msdb.dbo.syscategories WHERE name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]' AND category_class=1)
BEGIN
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_category @class=N'JOB', @type=N'LOCAL', @name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback

END

DECLARE @jobId BINARY(16)
EXEC @ReturnCode =  msdb.dbo.sp_add_job @job_name=N'Run CheckDB on database copy', 
		@enabled=1, 
		@notify_level_eventlog=0, 
		@notify_level_email=0, 
		@notify_level_netsend=0, 
		@notify_level_page=0, 
		@delete_level=0, 
		@description=N'No description available.', 
		@category_name=N'[Uncategorized (Local)]', 
		@owner_login_name=N'sa', @job_id = @jobId OUTPUT
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Check if we should start checkdb]    Script Date: 10/24/2017 2:22:03 PM ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Check if we should start checkdb', 
		@step_id=1, 
		@cmdexec_success_code=0, 
		@on_success_action=3, 
		@on_success_step_id=0, 
		@on_fail_action=1, 
		@on_fail_step_id=0, 
		@retry_attempts=0, 
		@retry_interval=0, 
		@os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
		@command=N'--First job step to check if next step should be run
use master
if not exists(select name from master.sys.databases where name = N''MHEUGT_CHECKDB'')
begin
	raiserror (N''MHEUGT_CHECKDB does not exist.'', 16,1)
end

use MHEUGT_CHECKDB
declare @check_status bit = 0
select @check_status = checkrunstatus from CHECK_STATUS
if (@check_status = 0)
begin
	raiserror (N''MHEUGT_CHECKDB not ready to run CheckDB.'', 16,1)
end

update CHECK_STATUS set checkrunstatus = 0', 
		@database_name=N'master', 
		@flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
/****** Object:  Step [Run CheckDB]    Script Date: 10/24/2017 2:22:03 PM ******/
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobstep @job_id=@jobId, @step_name=N'Run CheckDB', 
		@step_id=2, 
		@cmdexec_success_code=0, 
		@on_success_action=1, 
		@on_success_step_id=0, 
		@on_fail_action=2, 
		@on_fail_step_id=0, 
		@retry_attempts=0, 
		@retry_interval=0, 
		@os_run_priority=0, @subsystem=N'TSQL', 
		@command=N'--Next step run checkdb
DBCC CHECKDB(''MHEUGT_CHECKDB'')', 
		@database_name=N'master', 
		@flags=0
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_update_job @job_id = @jobId, @start_step_id = 1
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
EXEC @ReturnCode = msdb.dbo.sp_add_jobserver @job_id = @jobId, @server_name = N'(local)'
IF (@@ERROR <> 0 OR @ReturnCode <> 0) GOTO QuitWithRollback
COMMIT TRANSACTION
GOTO EndSave
QuitWithRollback:
    IF (@@TRANCOUNT > 0) ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
EndSave:

GO

Pause and wait for user input, cmdline and ISE
Function pause ($message)
{
    # Check if running Powershell ISE
    if ($psISE)
    {
        Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show("$message")
    }
    else
    {
        Write-Host "$message" -ForegroundColor Yellow
        $x = $host.ui.RawUI.ReadKey("NoEcho,IncludeKeyDown")
    }
}

# GLOBAL


$path = "C:\Temp\disks"
$serverfile = "$path\servers_GLOBAL.csv"
#$cred= get-credential 

Get-ADComputer -SearchBase "OU=Fms Servers,OU=Corporate Applications,OU=Managed Servers,DC=global,DC=scd,DC=scania,DC=com"  -Filter "Name -like 'SESOFMS*'"  -Properties * | Select -Property Name | Out-File "C:\Temp\disks\servers_GLOBAL.csv"

#Skip 3 first lines
#(get-content $empfile |Select-Object -Skip 3) | Set-Content $empfile


$Servers = Get-Content $serverfile

foreach ($server in $Servers) {
    
    if($server -like 'SESOFMS0*') {
        Write-host $server    
        $Disks = Get-wmiobject  Win32_LogicalDisk -computername $server -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue -filter "DriveType= 3 AND DeviceID != 'C:'" #-Credential $cred 
        $Servername = (Get-wmiobject  CIM_ComputerSystem -ComputerName $server).Name 
        $SpaceUsed = 0
        $TotalSpace = 0
        foreach ($objdisk in $Disks) {  
            $diskused = 0
            $out=New-Object PSObject 
            $total=“{0:N0}” -f ($objDisk.Size/1GB)  
            $free=($objDisk.FreeSpace/1GB)  
            #$freePercent=“{0:P0}” -f ([double]$objDisk.FreeSpace/[double]$objDisk.Size)  
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Servername" -Value $Servername 
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Drive" -Value $objDisk.DeviceID  
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Total size (GB)" -Value $total 
            $TotalSpace = $TotalSpace + $total
            $diskused = $total - $free
            $SpaceUsed = $SpaceUsed + $diskused
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name “Free Space (GB)” -Value $free 
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name “Free Space (%)” -Value $freePercent 
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Name " -Value $objdisk.volumename 
            #$out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "DriveType" -Value $objdisk.DriveType 
            #$out | export-csv $path\Diskspace_Report_GLobal.csv -NoTypeInformation -Append   
        }
        $out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Servername" -Value $ServerName 
        $out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Total Disk" -Value $TotalSpace
        $out | Add-Member -MemberType NoteProperty -Name "Used Disk" -Value $SpaceUsed
        $out | export-csv $path\Diskspace_Report_Used_XDS_9340.csv -NoTypeInformation -Append   
    }
}

Reporting Server
Systeminfo from database
The location of the installed and running Reporting Services instance is maybe not on the same server as the database instance. To find out where it is either check with:
select host_name, program_name, login_name, db_name(database_id) from sys.dm_exec_sessions where host_name is not null order by 1

or run: 
select * from ReportServer.dbo.Keys 

and check MachineName column.

Byta certifikat
netsh http show sslcert
netsh http delete sslcert ipport=[::]:443
netsh http delete sslcert ipport=0.0.0.0:443
netsh http show sslcert

samt ta bort bindingen som vart över i config toolet mot reports.xxx.x.x.x.x

klippte ut ulren i konfen och tog bort den i toolet också(säkert redundant)
<URL>
<UrlString>http://reports….</UrlString>
<AccountSid>S-1-5-21-2424351643-637275243-1735137507-1149</AccountSid>
<AccountName>VARDRAD\inesvcPIRSqlRpt</AccountName>
</URL>
